     1	# proposal — verify-change-scope
     2	
     3	**WHY**：多 change 并行的棕地 store 上，"本 change 达标"没有一等公民命令：`verify --change` 被历史 UNBOUND 淹没（副本实测 107 场景 61 UNBOUND），`--specs <delta>` 把别人的测试全判 ORPHAN（46 个误报）——达标要跑两个命令再人工解释，每个 change 重写一遍免责说明（复盘 roadmap P0-2）。根因：工具把"store 健康"与"本 change 达标"混在一个 verdict 里。
     4	
     5	**WHAT**：
     6	1. `verify --change` 的 verdict 收窄为"本 change 达标"：change 范围按 Requirement 块 provenance（ADDED/MODIFIED/RENAMED 产出块的场景 occurrence），REMOVED 不入；scoped duplicate（跨全投影撞 ID）与 scoped unidentified 计入 verdict。
     7	2. 同一次运行输出**完整** store report（六类：boundRed/unbound/true-orphan/unidentified/unattributed/duplicates）——历史缺口持续可见、零信息丢失，但不再伤害 verdict。
     8	3. D-SR-x 的 --change 限定：可解释的非零测试进程状态 + 无 infra error + change clean → GREEN exit 0（多 change 并行独立变绿的关键）；不可解释非零照旧 ERROR。
     9	4. gate C1（in-flight）消费收窄 verdict——并行 change 各自独立可绿；detail 带 store 摘要尾缀。
    10	5. 输出契约钉死：JSON 顶层改 change 语义、storeReport/changeScope 仅 GREEN/GAPS 存在（absent 非 null）、operations 数组表达 rename-then-modify、单次执行契约四路径表、零范围真值表。
    11	6. RUNBOOK 双语 STEP5 段、docs/cli 双语、CHANGELOG 三处行为变化声明。
    12	
    13	**OUT OF SCOPE**：`--specs` 与 post-archive 语义（byte 级不动）；三态定义本身；MODIFIED 完整性（change 3）；hotfix；跨 change 协调视图；archive/merge 语义；id-pattern 机制。
    14	
    15	**验收锚点**：req-final AC1–AC9；副本 batch3 对比证据（change 段 9 场景 vs 改前 107 场景淹没）。

 succeeded in 114ms:
     1	# TRUTH — spec-runner (`lib/spec-runner.js`, CLI subcommand `verify`)
     2	
     3	## Contract (code-is-truth)
     4	
     5	source-commit: d7d65d3
     6	
     7	**Responsibility.** Bind spec scenario IDs to a TAP-emitting test run; report per-scenario red/green; act as STEP5's deterministic gate. Fail-closed: a broken or vacuous run is never GREEN. 3.1: `--change <name>` verifies against the in-memory PROJECTED store (archive's merge() applied to the living store) — the mid-change form.
     8	
     9	**Public interface (module exports).**
    10	- `leadId(text, idRe)` → leading ID or null. Word-boundary strict: `XX-01b`, `XX-01_x` are NOT `XX-01`.
    11	- `stripFences(text)` → text with ``` fenced blocks removed (fenced scenarios are documentation, not spec).
    12	- `mdFiles` is internal; `collectScenarios(specTargets, idRe)` → `{ byId, unidentified:[[file,title]], files, missingTargets, duplicates:[{id,files}] }`. A target may be a dir (recursed for `*.md`, sorted) or a single file (accepted as-is, ANY extension); dedupes overlapping targets. Scenario discovery matches exact h4 lines `#### Scenario: …` only. Scenarios inside DEPRECATED requirement blocks (heading carries `_deprecated (superseded by …)_`) are excluded in EVERY form — not demanded, not listed; their lingering tests turn ORPHAN.
    13	- `collectScenariosFromTexts(Map<label,text>, idRe)` — same scan over in-memory texts (the projection path); labels are store-relative suffixes.
    14	- `parseTap(out, idRe)` → `{ results: Map<id,{pass,fail,skip}>, untagged:[], untaggedFails, bailout, plans:number[], points, dupNumbers:number[] }`. Only lines shaped exactly `ok|not ok <n> - <name>` BIND — a TAP result line without the ` - ` separator produces no binding. Since tap-plan (3.3.x) a per-line pre-pass (one trailing `\r` stripped) additionally collects: `plans` = every top-level `1..N` (trailing `# SKIP`/`# TODO` directive legal, N still parses); `points` = count of top-level result tokens `/^(?:ok|not ok)(?:\s|$)/` (bare/unnumbered points count; `ok:`-prefixed and indented lines never do); `dupNumbers` = complete numeric point tokens (`ok 1abc` stays unnumbered) seen more than once, numerically. `# SKIP`/`# TODO` counts as skip (proves nothing); untagged `not ok` without directive increments `untaggedFails`; `Bail out!` line captured.
    15	- `zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged)` → true when output exists but no TAP line parsed AND no `TAP version`/`1..N` plan line (plan line = TAP reporter with an empty suite, not a reporter problem).
    16	- `runTestCommand(cmd, cwd)` → `{ out (stdout+\n+stderr), status (null if signal-killed), signal, error }` via `spawnSync(shell:true)`.
    17	- `evaluate(byId, unidentified, results)` → `{ boundGreen, boundRed, unbound, orphan, unidentified, clean }`. A scenario whose only results are skips is UNBOUND.
    18	- `verify(opts {specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd})` — with `change`: builds the projection via archive-merge's discoverDeltas/buildProjection (roots `<cwd>/apriori/{changes,specs}`); ANY projection failure (validation/hygiene/CAS/merge-conflict) → run with `errors` set and NO test executed; success → normal pipeline over projected texts; run gains `projection: {change, modules, conflicts}`. Without `change`: unchanged. → run object `{ verdict, results, fileCount, failCount, scenarioCount, missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, noTap, exec{status,signal,error}, errors }`. `failCount` = tagged fails + `untaggedFails`.
    19	- `infraErrors(run)` → string[] (empty = trustworthy). Triggers: missing spec target · zero spec files · zero scenarios · spawn error · signal kill · bailout · zero-TAP-parsed · plan trio (defensive defaults keep legacy run shapes valid: multiple top-level plans → error naming the cure; exactly one plan and plan ≠ points → truncated/garbled; exactly one plan and duplicate point numbers) · non-zero exit with `failCount === 0` (TAP picture doesn't explain the exit). Gate surfaces any of these as ERROR exit 2 (`verify: <msg>`), outranking a C1 binding block; doctor's probe classification intentionally does not consume infraErrors.
    20	- `formatReport(...)` → human report; RESULT line: `ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)` / `GREEN — spec is the test suite` / `GAPS`. Duplicates block GREEN.
    21	- `verifyJson(run)` → `{ clean, result: GREEN|GAPS|ERROR, errors, specFiles, exec, duplicates, boundGreen[], boundRed[], unbound[], orphan[], unidentified[] }`; `--change` runs add exactly one field `projection: {change, modules, conflicts}` — never present otherwise. Since cas-enforcement (3.3.x): `--change` runs carry `projection.unstampedMutations` (suffixes, `[]` when none) + `projection.notes`; the cli prints one stderr warning per unstamped mutation delta — verdict untouched (WARN grade).
    22	- `configTestCmd(cwd)` → test-cmd from `apriori/process-config.md`'s `| test-cmd | … |` row, or null.
    23	- `cli(argv)` → exit code via the shared strict parser (truth/args.md): --help exits 0; unknown flags/stray positionals exit 2. `--specs <dir...>` OR `--change <name>` (mutually exclusive) `--test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern] [--cwd] [--json]`; test-cmd falls back to config row. Exit: 0 GREEN · 1 gaps/duplicates · 2 infra/usage error, incl. all projection failure classes.
    24	- `DEFAULT_ID` = `[A-Z]+-\d+`.
    25	
    26	**Data flow.** spec targets → collectScenarios → run test cmd once → parseTap → evaluate → infraErrors → report/JSON. One test-command invocation per verify run; no state persists between runs (stateless, reads FS + spawns only).
    27	
    28	**Three moments.** No external shared state. FS reads only; the spawned test command is the only side effect.
    29	
    30	**Pitfalls.**
    31	- `--specs` consumes following args until the next `--` flag — a path starting with `--` is impossible.
    32	- A direct-file `--specs` target is read whatever its extension; only directory recursion filters to `.md`.
    33	- `exec.status` is null when signal-killed; the non-zero-exit rule explicitly guards `status !== null`.
    34	- Requires `./archive-merge` (one-way: archive-merge never requires spec-runner).
    35	
    36	## Decisions (doc-is-truth)
    37	
    38	- D-SR-1 (active): fail-closed is a hard product stance — exit taxonomy 0/1/2; a vacuous or untrustworthy run must never exit 0. Ratified with 3.0.1 (owner + external review 2026-07-10).
    39	- D-SR-2 (active): skipped tests prove nothing — a scenario with only skips is UNBOUND, not green.
    40	- D-SR-3 (active): duplicate scenario IDs are a binding ambiguity → GAPS (exit 1), not a warning.
    41	- D-SR-4 (active): the binding gate speaks TAP only; non-TAP layers (e.g. Playwright E2E) sit above the gate as additional exit conditions, never replace it.
    42	- D-SR-5 (active): deprecated requirement blocks are excluded from scenario collection in ALL verify forms — post-REMOVED stores must not demand tests for removed behavior forever; lingering tests surface as ORPHAN. Ratified in change-projection (2026-07-10, STEP0 REQ-2).
    43	- D-SR-6 (active): mid-change, the STEP5 gate is `verify --change` (projection); plain `--specs` verify is the post-archive/store-only form. Ratified in change-projection.
    44	- D-SR-x (active): any top-level non-directive `not ok` blocks GREEN whatever its shape — unattributed failures are GAPS (exit 1) even on exit 0, infra ERRORs keep precedence, and a non-zero exec status never exits 0. Ratified in unattributed-fail (2026-07-12, GPT-5.6 review P0-1).
    45	- D-SR-y (active): TAP is parsed by a version-aware line lexer over stdout only — closed version matrix {12,13,14,absent}, escape-aware directive splitting, opaque fail-closed YAML blocks, pragma-only ignoring; stderr is a reported diagnostics channel, never parsed. Ratified in tap-contract (2026-07-12, GPT-5.6 fourth review).

     1	# req-final — verify-change-scope："本 change 达标"成为一等公民，历史缺口出 verdict 入报告
     2	
     3	target lineage: main（v4 产品线；不合并 v1/v3）
     4	
     5	## 背景（现状 A，实测证据）
     6	
     7	同 req-v1（副本复现：`--change` 107 场景 61 UNBOUND 2 ORPHAN 42 unattributed 淹没；`--specs <delta>` 46 ORPHAN 误报；"达标"需两命令+人工解释）。
     8	
     9	## 目标（目标态 B）
    10	
    11	### B1. change 范围：按 Requirement 块 provenance 定义（REQ-2 收口）
    12	
    13	- **change 块集** = 投影后 store 中，由本 delta 的 ADDED / MODIFIED / RENAMED 操作产出或改名的 **Requirement 块**（含 idempotent rerun 的 unchanged 路径——块内容相同仍属本 change 块集；REMOVED 产出的 deprecated 块不属于）。
    14	- **change 场景集（occurrence 级）** = change 块集内的全部场景 occurrence；其 ID 集用于测试归属。
    15	- **RENAMED 语义沿用 state A（REQ-1 收口）**：只改 Requirement 名，块内场景 ID 原样保留（SR-19 不动）；change scope 含改名后目标块的场景；场景 ID 变更必须由 MODIFIED 显式表达。
    16	- **本 change 测试集** = TAP 中 leading ID ∈ change 场景 ID 集的结果行。
    17	- **change verdict（exit 0/1 判据）**：change 场景每个 ≥1 passing、无 red；**duplicate**：任一 change 场景 ID 在整个投影中出现 >1 次（不论另一 occurrence 在不在 change 块集内）→ 不 GREEN（绑定歧义伤及本 change）；完全发生在 change 块集外的 duplicate → 只进 store report。**unidentified**：change 块集内的 unidentified occurrence → 计入 change verdict（不 GREEN）；块集外 → store report。
    18	
    19	### B2. store report：同一运行的**完整**信息性评估（REQ-3 收口）
    20	
    21	`--change` 输出两段：①change verdict 段（唯一判据）；②store report 段——对**整个投影 + 同一份 TAP 结果**的完整评估，六类全列：`boundRed` / `unbound` / true-`orphan`（leading ID 在整个投影中不存在的测试）/ `unidentified` / `unattributedFailures` / `duplicates`（各类给 count + 清单，human 输出沿用现行每类逐行、unattributed 现行 20 条截断规则；`boundGreen` 只给 count 不展开）。范围外 tagged red / duplicate / unidentified **不阻断** change verdict，但**必须**出现在 store report——不允许任何信息丢失。
    22	
    23	### B3. 单次执行契约（REQ-7 r2 收口：条件式，按路径分表）
    24	
    25	`verify --change` 的调用次数契约按四条路径分别钉死（state A 的 fail-early 顺序一律保持）：
    26	
    27	| 路径 | buildProjection | spec/delta 内容读取 | test spawn | TAP parse |
    28	|---|---|---|---|---|
    29	| pattern 非法（pre-resolve ERROR） | 0（仅 enumeration-only projection 元数据，state A 现状） | 0 | 0 | 0 |
    30	| 投影失败 / 标题批 matcher 失败 | 恰好 1 | 有（投影所需） | 0 | 0 |
    31	| 正常完成（GREEN/GAPS） | 恰好 1 | 有 | 恰好 1 | 恰好 1 |
    32	| post-TAP ERROR（bailout/plan mismatch/不可解释非零/TAP 批 matcher 失败） | 恰好 1 | 有 | 恰好 1 | 恰好 1 |
    33	
    34	两段（change verdict 与 store report）永远共享同一个成功投影、同一 resolved matcher、同一份 parsed TAP 快照——绝不为报告二跑测试。不新增 timeout/rollback 语义；matcher 的 2000ms 子进程预算与 fail-early 契约（上一 change）原样不动。
    35	
    36	### B4. exit 语义与 D-SR-x 的 --change 限定（REQ-5 收口）
    37	
    38	- infra ERROR（exit 2）类完全不变。
    39	- **仅对 `verify --change`**，非零 `exec.status` 的规则限定为：非零状态**可由已解析的失败解释**（范围内或范围外的 tagged red，或 unattributed failure——即 `failCount > 0`）且无 infra error 时，不再强制非 0 退出——change verdict clean 则 **GREEN exit 0**（范围外失败已完整呈现在 store report）；非零且 `failCount === 0`（无已解析失败可解释）→ 照旧 ERROR exit 2。
    40	- `--specs` 形式与 D-SR-x 原文完全不动；本限定作为 D-SR-x 的 scoped amendment 记入 CHANGELOG 与（归档时）truth Decisions。
    41	
    42	### B5. 零范围真值表（REQ-6 收口）
    43	
    44	| 情形 | 判定 |
    45	|---|---|
    46	| change scope 空 + delta 仅含 REMOVED + 投影非空 | change verdict **GREEN（vacuous-by-design）**，标注 `0 scenario(s) in change scope (removal-only change)`；REMOVED 场景的 lingering 测试进 store report 的 orphan |
    47	| change scope 空 + delta 含 ADDED/MODIFIED/RENAMED（产出块无场景，可与 REMOVED 混合）+ 投影非空 | 同样 **GREEN（vacuous-by-design）**，标注为通用文案 `0 scenario(s) in change scope` 尾随实际操作摘要（如 `(ops: ADDED 1, REMOVED 1)`）——**不得**误标 removal-only |
    48	| change scope 空 + 整个投影零场景 | **ERROR exit 2**（沿用 D-SR-1 全局 vacuous 拒绝——投影空是 store 级 infra 事实） |
    49	| change 块集内只有 unidentified 场景（scope 的 ID 集空但 occurrence 非空） | **GAPS exit 1**（unidentified 计入 change verdict，B1） |
    50	| delta 零操作/malformed | 现行投影失败路径不变（ERROR exit 2） |
    51	
    52	D-SR-1 的全局面（投影零场景/零文件 → ERROR）原样保留。

    54	### B6. 输出契约定稿（REQ-4/Q1 收口）
    55	
    56	- **JSON（`--change` 运行）**：顶层 `clean` / `result` / `boundGreen` / `boundRed` / `unbound` / `orphan` / `unidentified` / `unattributedFailures` / **`duplicates`** 全部**改为 change 语义**（REQ-4 r2：`duplicates` 明确在列——change 范围 duplicate；full-projection duplicates 只存在于 `storeReport.duplicates`）。数组形状统一裁定：**数组的 `length` 即 count**，不引入 `{count, items}` 包装（唯一例外：`unattributedFailures` 保持既有 `{count, lines}`；`storeReport.boundGreen` 是纯 count 数字）。新增字段 `storeReport`：**当且仅当 `result ∈ {GREEN, GAPS}` 存在**；一切 ERROR 类（pre-test 与 post-TAP 皆然）**一律缺省**——fail-closed：不可信运行的绑定数据不作为报告呈现（存在性矩阵：GREEN✓ GAPS✓ ERROR✗）。`projection` 形状不变；`specFiles`/`exec` 属整个投影/运行不变。`--specs` 运行的 JSON 完全不变（无 storeReport）。
    57	- **模块 API（`verify()` run 对象）**：`run.verdict`、`run.duplicates` 改按 change 语义（--change 时）；新增 `run.storeReport` 与 `run.changeScope`——两者的存在性规则统一为：**当且仅当 `result ∈ {GREEN, GAPS}` 时字段存在；一切 ERROR 类与 `--specs` 运行中字段一律缺省（absent，绝非 null）**。`run.changeScope = { requirements: [...], scenarioIds: [...]（排序） }`，其中 requirements 元素为 `{file, name, operations: [...]}`——**operations 是数组**（REQ-8）：按 merge 执行顺序（RENAMED→ADDED→MODIFIED→REMOVED）列出该最终块经历的全部操作（如 rename-then-modify 的 `Gamma` 块 = `{file, name:"Gamma", operations:["RENAMED","MODIFIED"]}`）；RENAMED+REMOVED 的最终 deprecated 块不属于 change scope（provenance 以最终投影状态为准）；requirements 按 (file, name) 排序。`formatReport` 接受可选 storeReport 段渲染。AC 需为 GREEN / GAPS / pre-test ERROR / post-TAP ERROR 四类各给一个完整 JSON oracle。
    58	- **gate C1（in-flight）**：消费 change 语义 verdict 与 change 范围 duplicates；detail 变为 `verify GREEN (in-flight, change-scoped)` / `verify GAPS: <change 范围各类计数>`，尾缀追加 `; store: <boundRed>/<unbound>/<orphan>/<unattributed> outstanding`（纯文本摘要，JSON 的 checks[].detail 仍是字符串——gate JSON shape 不变）。archived 分支不动。

    69

    71	- AC2（change 缺口）：XC-01 无测试 → GAPS exit 1 且 unbound=[XC-01]（change 段）；XC-01 not ok → GAPS 且 boundRed=[XC-01]。

     1	# TRUTH — gate (`lib/gate.js`, CLI subcommand `gate`)
     2	
     3	## Contract (code-is-truth)
     4	
     5	source-commit: 83fd13e
     6	
     7	**Responsibility.** Aggregate the MECHANICAL exit conditions for one change into one exit code: 0 PASS · 1 BLOCKED · 2 evaluation untrustworthy. Strictly read-only. PASS covers machine checks only — human gates remain human (stated in output).
     8	
     9	**Public interface.**
    10	- `runGate({cwd, change, testCmd})` → `{ code, stage: 'in-flight'|'archived'|null, checks:[{id,status:'pass'|'blocked'|'n/a',detail}], result:'PASS'|'BLOCKED'|'ERROR', blocked, errors, change }`.
    11	- `resolveChange(cwd, name)` → `{stage, dir}` or `{error}` — in-flight `apriori/changes/<name>` first (realpath containment vs changes dir); else archived: basenames matching `/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{4}-<name>$/` under `changes/archive/` that stat as DIRECTORIES, sorted, last; containment vs the ARCHIVE root (a symlink pointing elsewhere under changes/ is an escape).
    12	- `cli(argv)`: `--change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--cwd <dir>] [--json / --no-cas (C7 waiver, visible in the check detail)]` via the shared strict parser (truth/args.md); testCmd falls back to `configTestCmd(cwd)`; neither → exit 2. `--json` = pure JSON in EVERY outcome class incl. parse errors (the jsonError seam).
    13	
    14	**The seven checks.** C1 binding (in-flight → `verify({change})` projected; archived → `verify({specs:[apriori/specs]})`; verify infra errors → gate exit 2) · C2 tasks.md zero `- [ ]` (`[x]`/`[X]` checked; absent → n/a on trivial, blocked otherwise) · C3 flow-state legality (required keys change/tier/track/lineage/current-step; placeholder = contains `<`/`>`; exact step/tier enums; change must equal --change; file missing → exit 2) · C4 ledger at `<dir>/review/issues.md` (the resolved change dir's bundle) BOTH stages, speaking the terminal-state vocabulary since ledger-states (3.3.x): statuses classified by `classifyStatus` (exported; leading token case-insensitive, `rejected-verified` alternated before `rejected`) — unknown statuses block at both stages; `open` blocks; `rejected`/`rejected-verified`/`waived` need a word character after the token; a `waived` row needs same-entry human evidence in the change's flow-state `gates:` block (one entry carrying the row ID as an exact token — LS-1 never matches inside LS-10 — plus /waiv/i); at the ARCHIVED stage every row must be terminal (`verified`/`rejected-verified`/`waived`/`advisory-acked`) — `fixed` and plain `rejected` block with reviewer-verify/concur-or-human-waive cures; in-flight `fixed` and reasoned `rejected` pass (absent ledger → n/a on trivial) · C5 verdict evidence (docs `<dir>/review/*.md`, minus `issues.md` and raw stems, regular files only — symlinked doc blocks; `/^VERDICT:/m` doc needs a REGULAR (lstat-first) `<dir>/review/<stem>-raw.*` beside it). Shared C4/C5 evidence-root guard `reviewDirDefect(dir)`: `<dir>/review` is lstat-ed FIRST (only ENOENT is absence — a dangling symlink is a defect); symlink / non-dir / escape (containsReal) blocks BOTH C4 and C5 naming the defect · C6 KB freshness (modules = first segments of delta-spec suffixes; precondition ladder → n/a with reason; `git -C <cwd> log --oneline <stamp>..HEAD -- lib/<m>.js`: clean pass / commits block / non-zero git exit n/a). · C7 CAS default-deny (in-flight: the projection's `unstampedMutations` block naming each suffix + the stamp cure; waivers are VISIBLE — `--no-cas` flag or a `| cas | optional |` process-config row (flag wins, `configCas` leading value token case-insensitive); archived → n/a, deltas already merged; neither waiver touches verify/archive behavior)
    15	
    16	**Pitfalls.**
    17	- C1 runs the project's full test command — gate's runtime is dominated by it.
    18	- C6's module→code mapping is the `lib/<m>.js` convention; anything off-convention degrades to n/a (never a false block).
    19	- GT-12's read-only test fingerprints path+size+mtime — same-size rewrites with restored mtime would evade it; static inspection is the actual guarantee (gate performs zero writes).
    20	- Requires `./status`, `./spec-runner`, `./archive-merge`; nothing requires gate (leaf module).

    23	
    24	- D-GT-1 (active): gate judges the MECHANICAL face only; its PASS never substitutes for protected human gates — the output must say so. Ratified in gate-command (2026-07-11).
    25	- D-GT-2 (active): infra failures (git absent, bad stamp commit, unmappable module) yield n/a with a reason, never a fabricated block — false blocks train humans to ignore the gate.

     1	# design — verify-change-scope
     2	
     3	## 总体：全部改动以 `opts.change` 分支门控，`--specs` 路径零触碰（AC7 R1）
     4	
     5	## G1 buildChangeProjection 附带 scope 元数据
     6	
     7	`buildChangeProjection`（spec-runner 内部）在既有返回上增加 `scopeOps: Map<suffix, {added:[name], modified:[name], renamedPairs:[[old,new]], removed:[name]}>`——由 `am.discoverDeltas` 的文件重跑 `am.parseDelta`（投影已做过 hygiene 校验，此处只取桶；零改 archive-merge）。失败路径（b.texts 为 null）不需要 scopeOps。
     8	
     9	## G2 scope 计算（新内部函数 `computeChangeScope(texts, scopeOps)`）
    10	
    11	对每个 delta suffix：
    12	1. `blocks = am.parseRequirementsStrict(texts.get(suffix)).map`（投影后文本）。
    13	2. 候选名 = added ∪ modified ∪ renamedPairs.new；排除最终为 deprecated 的块（rename-then-remove：REMOVED 命中 renamed new name 时块名带 deprecated 后缀，直接查 blocks 中同名非 deprecated 是否存在）。
    14	3. 每个入选块：`operations` 数组按 merge 序（RENAMED→ADDED→MODIFIED）由三个桶的命中拼出；`scanTitles(blockText)` 收集 occurrence（复用既有结构收集）。
    15	4. 汇总 → `changeScope = { requirements: [{file: suffix, name, operations}] 按 (file,name) 排序, scenarioIds: 排序去重 }` + `scopedPairs`（occurrence 级 [label,title]）。
    16	
    17	## G3 verdict 拆分（verify() --change 收尾段重构）
    18	
    19	既有流程不变至 TAP parse 完成，然后：
    20	- **全投影集合**（现 collected）继续算，产出 `storeEval = evaluate(byId_full, unidentified_full, results, unattributedFailures)` + duplicates_full → **run.storeReport**：`{ boundGreen: storeEval.boundGreen.length, boundRed, unbound, orphan, unidentified, unattributedFailures: {count, lines}, duplicates: duplicates_full }`（orphan 定义不变——leading ID 不在全投影 byId 即 true orphan）。
    21	- **scoped 绑定**：`bindPairs(scopedPairs, matcher)`——同一 matcher；但**不再二次调用 matcher**：scopedPairs 的标题是全投影标题的子集，从已匹配的全投影结果复用（实现上：全投影 bindPairs 时保留 pairs→ids 对照表，scoped 直接查表；保证单次执行契约 B3——matcher.batch 对标题批只跑一次）。
    22	- **change verdict**：`verdict.boundGreen/boundRed/unbound` 由 scoped byId × results 计算（evaluate 的裁剪版或直接 evaluate(byId_scoped, unidentified_scoped, results) 后丢弃其 orphan）；`run.duplicates`（change 语义）= scoped ID 在全投影 occurrence 计数 >1 的项；`verdict.orphan = []`（--change 下恒空，true orphan 只在 storeReport）；`verdict.unidentified = unidentified_scoped`；`verdict.unattributed = []`（--change 下不入 verdict）。clean = 无 red/unbound/scoped-unidentified/scoped-duplicate。
    23	- **零范围**：scopedPairs 空时按 B5 真值表：投影场景全空 → 既有全局 vacuous ERROR 不动（infraErrors 已有 zero-scenarios 规则——注意该规则看全投影，保持）；否则 vacuous GREEN，`run.vacuousNote = '0 scenario(s) in change scope' + (removal-only ? ' (removal-only change)' : ' (ops: …)')`。
    24	- **exit**：cli 的 `run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1` 判据文本不变——语义随字段收窄自动生效；infraErrors 不改（"可解释非零"本就不在 infra 清单）。
    25	- run.changeScope / run.storeReport 仅在 GREEN/GAPS 路径赋值（errorsRun 恒不带——absent 非 null）。
    26	
    27	## G4 输出

    30	- `verifyJson`：--change 且非 ERROR → 顶层数组换 scoped 数据 + `storeReport`/`changeScope` 字段；ERROR → 现状（不带两字段）；--specs → 现状。
    31	- gate `checkBinding`：in-flight 用 run.verdict（已 scoped）+ run.duplicates（已 scoped）；detail：pass=`verify GREEN (in-flight, change-scoped)${storeSuffix}`；block=`verify GAPS: <scoped 各类计数>${storeSuffix}`；`storeSuffix = '; store: R red, U unbound, O orphan, N unidentified, A unattributed, D duplicate(s) outstanding'`（从 run.storeReport 取；无 storeReport（ERROR）时走既有 infra 通道）。既有 GT-01 等断言检查 `verify GREEN (in-flight)` 前缀——detail 文案变化需同步既有测试（gap R4）。

     2	
     3	## ADDED Requirements
     4	
     5	### Requirement: change-scoped verify separates the change verdict from the store report
     6	On a `--change` run, `verify` SHALL judge its verdict (exit 0/1) on the CHANGE SCOPE only, while reporting the whole projection informatively. The change scope is defined by requirement-block provenance over the projection: the blocks produced or renamed by the delta's ADDED / MODIFIED / RENAMED operations (idempotent reruns included; blocks whose final projected state is deprecated — including rename-then-remove — are excluded), with their scenario occurrences collected at occurrence level. `RENAMED` keeps its store semantics (requirement name only; scenario IDs preserved). The change verdict is GREEN iff every scoped scenario has ≥1 passing test and none red, no scoped occurrence is unidentified, and no scoped scenario ID occurs more than once ACROSS THE WHOLE PROJECTION (a cross-boundary collision is a binding ambiguity of this change; collisions entirely outside the scope go to the store report only). Out-of-scope tests are never ORPHAN for the verdict; a test whose leading ID exists nowhere in the projection is a true orphan, reported in the store report. The store report is a COMPLETE informative evaluation of the whole projection against the same TAP snapshot: `boundRed`, `unbound`, true-`orphan`, `unidentified`, `unattributedFailures`, `duplicates` (each a full list whose length is its count; `boundGreen` as a bare count) — out-of-scope reds, duplicates and unidentified MUST stay visible there. Execution follows a conditional single-run contract: invalid pattern → 0 projection builds / 0 content reads / 0 test spawns / 0 TAP parses (the fail-early order stands); projection or title-batch matcher failure → exactly 1 projection, 0 spawns; the normal and post-TAP-error paths → exactly 1 projection, 1 spawn, 1 parse; both views share the one projection and the one parsed TAP snapshot — the report never re-runs tests. Exit semantics: infra ERROR classes are unchanged; solely for `--change`, a non-zero test-process status that is EXPLAINED by parsed failures (`failCount > 0`) with no infra error no longer forces a non-zero exit — a clean change verdict is GREEN exit 0 (a scoped amendment to the non-zero rule; an unexplained non-zero stays ERROR exit 2). `--specs` runs are byte-identical to before.
     7	
     8	#### Scenario: SR-56 the change verdict judges only the change scope
     9	- WHEN the store has `R-A`(XA-01) and `R-B`(XB-01), the delta ADDs `R-C`(XC-01), and the test command prints `ok XC-01`, `ok XA-01`, `ok XZ-99`, one ID-less `not ok`, then exits 1
    10	- THEN `verify --change` is GREEN exit 0; the store report carries unbound=[XB-01], orphan=[XZ-99], unattributedFailures.count=1, boundRed=[]; and the same fixture through `--specs apriori/specs` behaves exactly as today (GAPS)
    11	
    12	#### Scenario: SR-57 change gaps still fail
    13	- WHEN XC-01 has no test (and separately: its only test is `not ok`)
    14	- THEN the run is GAPS exit 1 with the change verdict carrying unbound=[XC-01] (respectively boundRed=[XC-01])
    15	
    16	#### Scenario: SR-58 operation semantics bound the scope
    17	- WHEN a delta MODIFIES a block to contain XA-01 and XA-02, REMOVEs a block whose scenario still has a lingering test, RENAMEs `R-A -> R-G` (block contains XA-01), and separately renames-then-modifies (`RENAMED R-A -> R-G` + `MODIFIED R-G`) and renames-then-removes
    18	- THEN the modified block's XA-01 and XA-02 are both in scope; the removed block's scenario is not demanded and its lingering test lands in the store report's orphan; the renamed block's XA-01 stays in scope under its unchanged ID; the rename-then-modify final block is in scope with `operations: ["RENAMED","MODIFIED"]`; the rename-then-remove final deprecated block is out of scope
    19	
    20	#### Scenario: SR-59 in-scope strictness blocks the verdict
    21	- WHEN a scoped block contains an unidentified scenario, or a scoped scenario ID also occurs in an untouched store block
    22	- THEN the change verdict is not GREEN (scoped unidentified and cross-boundary duplicates count); a duplicate entirely outside the scope leaves the verdict GREEN-able and appears in the store report's duplicates
    23	
    24	#### Scenario: SR-60 the store report loses nothing
    25	- WHEN out-of-scope tests are red, an out-of-scope duplicate exists, and an out-of-scope block carries an unidentified scenario
    26	- THEN none of them blocks the change verdict, and every one of them appears in the store report (boundRed / duplicates / unidentified respectively)
    27	
    28	#### Scenario: SR-61 explained non-zero status can be GREEN, unexplained stays ERROR
    29	- WHEN the test command exits 1 with parsed failures that explain it (out-of-scope red or unattributed) and the change verdict is clean
    30	- THEN the run is GREEN exit 0; and WHEN the command exits non-zero with `failCount === 0` THEN the run stays ERROR exit 2 (the unexplained-non-zero rule is untouched)
    31	
    32	#### Scenario: SR-62 the zero-scope truth table holds
    33	- WHEN the change scope is empty
    34	- THEN a pure-REMOVED delta over a non-empty projection is GREEN with the note `0 scenario(s) in change scope (removal-only change)`; a mixed/empty-block delta (ADDED/MODIFIED/RENAMED yielding no scenarios) is GREEN with the generic note plus an ops summary and never the removal-only wording; an all-empty projection is ERROR exit 2 (the global vacuous rule); a scope whose only occurrences are unidentified is GAPS exit 1; a zero-op or malformed delta keeps today's projection-failure ERROR
    35	
    36	#### Scenario: SR-63 one projection, one test run, one parse
    37	- WHEN a `--change` run completes (normally or with a post-TAP error) with a sentinel test command that counts its spawns
    38	- THEN the sentinel counted exactly one spawn, exactly one projection was built, and the change verdict and store report agree on the same TAP snapshot; the invalid-pattern and projection-failure paths spawn nothing (existing assertions)
    39	
    40	#### Scenario: SR-64 the JSON contract is pinned for all four outcome classes
    41	- WHEN `--change --json` runs end GREEN, GAPS, pre-test ERROR, and post-TAP ERROR
    42	- THEN GREEN/GAPS carry top-level change-scoped `clean/result/boundGreen/boundRed/unbound/orphan/unidentified/unattributedFailures/duplicates` plus `storeReport` (six classes; array length = count; `boundGreen` a bare count; `unattributedFailures` keeps `{count, lines}`) and `changeScope` (`requirements` as sorted `{file, name, operations[]}`, `scenarioIds` sorted); BOTH ERROR classes omit `storeReport` and `changeScope` entirely (absent, never null); `projection` keeps its existing shape; a `--specs --json` run is unchanged and never carries either field

    11	### B1. change 范围：按 Requirement 块 provenance 定义（REQ-2 收口）
    12	
    13	- **change 块集** = 投影后 store 中，由本 delta 的 ADDED / MODIFIED / RENAMED 操作产出或改名的 **Requirement 块**（含 idempotent rerun 的 unchanged 路径——块内容相同仍属本 change 块集；REMOVED 产出的 deprecated 块不属于）。
    14	- **change 场景集（occurrence 级）** = change 块集内的全部场景 occurrence；其 ID 集用于测试归属。
    15	- **RENAMED 语义沿用 state A（REQ-1 收口）**：只改 Requirement 名，块内场景 ID 原样保留（SR-19 不动）；change scope 含改名后目标块的场景；场景 ID 变更必须由 MODIFIED 显式表达。
    16	- **本 change 测试集** = TAP 中 leading ID ∈ change 场景 ID 集的结果行。
    17	- **change verdict（exit 0/1 判据）**：change 场景每个 ≥1 passing、无 red；**duplicate**：任一 change 场景 ID 在整个投影中出现 >1 次（不论另一 occurrence 在不在 change 块集内）→ 不 GREEN（绑定歧义伤及本 change）；完全发生在 change 块集外的 duplicate → 只进 store report。**unidentified**：change 块集内的 unidentified occurrence → 计入 change verdict（不 GREEN）；块集外 → store report。
    18	
    19	### B2. store report：同一运行的**完整**信息性评估（REQ-3 收口）
    20	
    21	`--change` 输出两段：①change verdict 段（唯一判据）；②store report 段——对**整个投影 + 同一份 TAP 结果**的完整评估，六类全列：`boundRed` / `unbound` / true-`orphan`（leading ID 在整个投影中不存在的测试）/ `unidentified` / `unattributedFailures` / `duplicates`（各类给 count + 清单，human 输出沿用现行每类逐行、unattributed 现行 20 条截断规则；`boundGreen` 只给 count 不展开）。范围外 tagged red / duplicate / unidentified **不阻断** change verdict，但**必须**出现在 store report——不允许任何信息丢失。
    22	
    23	### B3. 单次执行契约（REQ-7 r2 收口：条件式，按路径分表）
    24	
    25	`verify --change` 的调用次数契约按四条路径分别钉死（state A 的 fail-early 顺序一律保持）：
    26	
    27	| 路径 | buildProjection | spec/delta 内容读取 | test spawn | TAP parse |
    28	|---|---|---|---|---|
    29	| pattern 非法（pre-resolve ERROR） | 0（仅 enumeration-only projection 元数据，state A 现状） | 0 | 0 | 0 |
    30	| 投影失败 / 标题批 matcher 失败 | 恰好 1 | 有（投影所需） | 0 | 0 |
    31	| 正常完成（GREEN/GAPS） | 恰好 1 | 有 | 恰好 1 | 恰好 1 |
    32	| post-TAP ERROR（bailout/plan mismatch/不可解释非零/TAP 批 matcher 失败） | 恰好 1 | 有 | 恰好 1 | 恰好 1 |
    33	
    34	两段（change verdict 与 store report）永远共享同一个成功投影、同一 resolved matcher、同一份 parsed TAP 快照——绝不为报告二跑测试。不新增 timeout/rollback 语义；matcher 的 2000ms 子进程预算与 fail-early 契约（上一 change）原样不动。
    35	
    36	### B4. exit 语义与 D-SR-x 的 --change 限定（REQ-5 收口）
    37	
    38	- infra ERROR（exit 2）类完全不变。
    39	- **仅对 `verify --change`**，非零 `exec.status` 的规则限定为：非零状态**可由已解析的失败解释**（范围内或范围外的 tagged red，或 unattributed failure——即 `failCount > 0`）且无 infra error 时，不再强制非 0 退出——change verdict clean 则 **GREEN exit 0**（范围外失败已完整呈现在 store report）；非零且 `failCount === 0`（无已解析失败可解释）→ 照旧 ERROR exit 2。
    40	- `--specs` 形式与 D-SR-x 原文完全不动；本限定作为 D-SR-x 的 scoped amendment 记入 CHANGELOG 与（归档时）truth Decisions。
    41	
    42	### B5. 零范围真值表（REQ-6 收口）
    43	
    44	| 情形 | 判定 |
    45	|---|---|
    46	| change scope 空 + delta 仅含 REMOVED + 投影非空 | change verdict **GREEN（vacuous-by-design）**，标注 `0 scenario(s) in change scope (removal-only change)`；REMOVED 场景的 lingering 测试进 store report 的 orphan |
    47	| change scope 空 + delta 含 ADDED/MODIFIED/RENAMED（产出块无场景，可与 REMOVED 混合）+ 投影非空 | 同样 **GREEN（vacuous-by-design）**，标注为通用文案 `0 scenario(s) in change scope` 尾随实际操作摘要（如 `(ops: ADDED 1, REMOVED 1)`）——**不得**误标 removal-only |
    48	| change scope 空 + 整个投影零场景 | **ERROR exit 2**（沿用 D-SR-1 全局 vacuous 拒绝——投影空是 store 级 infra 事实） |
    49	| change 块集内只有 unidentified 场景（scope 的 ID 集空但 occurrence 非空） | **GAPS exit 1**（unidentified 计入 change verdict，B1） |
    50	| delta 零操作/malformed | 现行投影失败路径不变（ERROR exit 2） |
    51	
    52	D-SR-1 的全局面（投影零场景/零文件 → ERROR）原样保留。
    53	
    54	### B6. 输出契约定稿（REQ-4/Q1 收口）
    55	
    56	- **JSON（`--change` 运行）**：顶层 `clean` / `result` / `boundGreen` / `boundRed` / `unbound` / `orphan` / `unidentified` / `unattributedFailures` / **`duplicates`** 全部**改为 change 语义**（REQ-4 r2：`duplicates` 明确在列——change 范围 duplicate；full-projection duplicates 只存在于 `storeReport.duplicates`）。数组形状统一裁定：**数组的 `length` 即 count**，不引入 `{count, items}` 包装（唯一例外：`unattributedFailures` 保持既有 `{count, lines}`；`storeReport.boundGreen` 是纯 count 数字）。新增字段 `storeReport`：**当且仅当 `result ∈ {GREEN, GAPS}` 存在**；一切 ERROR 类（pre-test 与 post-TAP 皆然）**一律缺省**——fail-closed：不可信运行的绑定数据不作为报告呈现（存在性矩阵：GREEN✓ GAPS✓ ERROR✗）。`projection` 形状不变；`specFiles`/`exec` 属整个投影/运行不变。`--specs` 运行的 JSON 完全不变（无 storeReport）。
    57	- **模块 API（`verify()` run 对象）**：`run.verdict`、`run.duplicates` 改按 change 语义（--change 时）；新增 `run.storeReport` 与 `run.changeScope`——两者的存在性规则统一为：**当且仅当 `result ∈ {GREEN, GAPS}` 时字段存在；一切 ERROR 类与 `--specs` 运行中字段一律缺省（absent，绝非 null）**。`run.changeScope = { requirements: [...], scenarioIds: [...]（排序） }`，其中 requirements 元素为 `{file, name, operations: [...]}`——**operations 是数组**（REQ-8）：按 merge 执行顺序（RENAMED→ADDED→MODIFIED→REMOVED）列出该最终块经历的全部操作（如 rename-then-modify 的 `Gamma` 块 = `{file, name:"Gamma", operations:["RENAMED","MODIFIED"]}`）；RENAMED+REMOVED 的最终 deprecated 块不属于 change scope（provenance 以最终投影状态为准）；requirements 按 (file, name) 排序。`formatReport` 接受可选 storeReport 段渲染。AC 需为 GREEN / GAPS / pre-test ERROR / post-TAP ERROR 四类各给一个完整 JSON oracle。
    58	- **gate C1（in-flight）**：消费 change 语义 verdict 与 change 范围 duplicates；detail 变为 `verify GREEN (in-flight, change-scoped)` / `verify GAPS: <change 范围各类计数>`，尾缀追加 `; store: <boundRed>/<unbound>/<orphan>/<unattributed> outstanding`（纯文本摘要，JSON 的 checks[].detail 仍是字符串——gate JSON shape 不变）。archived 分支不动。
    59	
    60	### B7. 文档同步（Q3 收口：最小幅度）
    61	
    62	RUNBOOK.md/RUNBOOK_cn.md §4 STEP5 投影段改写两段式语义（verdict=change 达标；store report=信息性），验证矩阵处加一句两视角；runbook-version 不动（4.0 内行为细化，CLI major 未变——评审可再裁）。docs/cli*.md verify/gate 节；CHANGELOG 显式声明三处行为变化（--change verdict 收窄、JSON 字段语义、D-SR-x 限定）。

    68	## 验收标准（确定 oracle）
    69	
    70	- AC1（一等公民）：fixture——store `### R-A`(XA-01)+`### R-B`(XB-01)；delta ADDED `### R-C`(XC-01)；test-cmd 输出 `ok XC-01`、`ok XA-01`、`ok XZ-99`、`not ok`（无 ID）后 `process.exit(1)`。`verify --change` → **GREEN exit 0**；storeReport：unbound=[XB-01]、orphan=[XZ-99]、unattributed.count=1、boundRed=[]；`--json` 顶层 clean=true 且 storeReport 恒在。（对照断言：同 fixture 在 `--specs apriori/specs` 下现行为不变——GAPS。）
    71	- AC2（change 缺口）：XC-01 无测试 → GAPS exit 1 且 unbound=[XC-01]（change 段）；XC-01 not ok → GAPS 且 boundRed=[XC-01]。
    72	- AC3（操作语义）：MODIFIED 替换后块含 XA-01+XA-02 → 两者皆 in scope；REMOVED 块场景不被要求、其 lingering 测试 → storeReport.orphan；RENAMED `R-A -> R-G`（块含 XA-01）→ XA-01 in scope 且 ID 不变（SR-19 兼容）；**rename-then-modify**（`RENAMED R-A -> R-G` + `MODIFIED R-G`）→ 最终 `R-G` 块 in scope 且 `changeScope.requirements` 含 `operations:["RENAMED","MODIFIED"]`；rename-then-remove → 最终 deprecated 块不入 scope。
    73	- AC4（范围内严格）：change 块内 unidentified → 不 GREEN；change 场景 ID 与块外 store 场景撞 ID → 不 GREEN（duplicate 进 change verdict）；纯块外 duplicate → GREEN 可达 + storeReport.duplicates 列出。
    74	- AC5（gate 独立变绿）：两个活 change（场景/测试互不重叠，另有一红测试属 change-B 范围）→ change-A 的 `gate --change` C1 pass（detail 含 change-scoped 与 store 摘要尾缀）、change-B 的 C1 blocked；同一 fixture 下测试命令 exit 1 不影响 change-A 的 C1（B4 限定生效）。
    75	- AC6（真实样本对比，证据入 review/，含不可变身份）：副本 batch3——改前 `--change`＝107 场景 61 UNBOUND 2 ORPHAN 42 unattributed GAPS；改后 change 段仅 batch3 的 9 场景（2 green/7 unbound → GAPS，缺口属于本 change），storeReport 计数与改前全局一致（61/2/42 保持可见）；无需再跑 `--specs <delta>`。
    76	- AC7（兼容）：`--specs` 一切形式 byte 级行为不变；`--change` infra ERROR 类不变（含"非零且 failCount=0 → ERROR"反例断言）；B3 四路径调用次数契约各有断言（pattern 非法路径 0 projection/0 read/0 spawn/0 parse——沿用上一 change 的既有断言即可引用；正常路径 sentinel 恰好 1 次 spawn）；JSON 四类 oracle（GREEN/GAPS/pre-test ERROR/post-TAP ERROR——后者断言 storeReport 缺省）；存量测试保持绿（数量不钉死，现 284）。
    77	- AC8（零范围真值表）：B5 五行各有对应断言（removal-only GREEN + 专属文案；混合空范围 GREEN + 通用文案含 ops 摘要且不含 removal-only 字样；空投影 ERROR；scoped-unidentified GAPS；malformed 不变）。
    78	- AC9（文档）：RUNBOOK 双语 STEP5 段 + 验证矩阵一句、docs/cli 双语 verify/gate 节、CHANGELOG 三处变化声明；`check --self` 绿。

548 lib/spec-runner.js
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * spec-runner — bind spec scenario IDs to test runs, report per-scenario red/green.
     4	 * VISION endgame, weak form: "spec IS the test suite". Zero deps — pure Node stdlib.
     5	 * Coupling surface is tiny and language-agnostic: markdown scenarios + a TAP-emitting test command.
     6	 *
     7	 * FAIL-CLOSED: a deterministic gate must refuse to say GREEN when its inputs are missing,
     8	 * its instrument misbehaved, or its evidence is ambiguous. Vacuous success is a bug.
     9	 * Exit codes: 0 = GREEN · 1 = spec/test gaps · 2 = invocation or infrastructure error.
    10	 */
    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const path = require('path');
    13	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    14	// merge-family helpers shared with archive (3.1 projection); archive-merge never requires spec-runner
    15	const am = require('./archive-merge');
    16	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    17	// DEFAULT_ID lives in config (the zero-dep resolution home) and is re-exported here unchanged
    18	const { DEFAULT_ID, resolveIdPattern, sanitizeMsg } = require('./config');
    19	const SCENARIO_RE = /^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/gm;
    20	// TAP result line, directive split off separately (# SKIP / # TODO)
    21	const TAP_RE = /^(ok|not ok)\s+\d+\s+-\s+(.*)$/gm;
    22	
    23	// Leading scenario/test ID. The match must end at a word boundary: `XX-01b` is NOT `XX-01` —
    24	// silently truncating would merge two different scenarios into one binding.
    25	function leadId(text, idRe) {
    26	  const m = text.trim().match(idRe);
    27	  if (!m || m.index !== 0) return null;
    28	  const next = text.trim()[m[0].length];
    29	  if (next !== undefined && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(next)) return null;
    30	  return m[0];
    31	}
    32	
    33	function stripFences(text) { return text.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ''); }
    34	
    35	// ---- the id matcher: one recognition contract, two execution channels -------------------
    36	// A config-sourced pattern is repository input that CI consumes automatically; its every
    37	// actual application runs inside a terminable child (SIGKILL on budget) so a catastrophic
    38	// pattern can never hang the CLI. Flag/default origins are operator/built-in trust: inline.
    39	const CHILD_PATH = path.join(__dirname, 'id-match-child.js');
    40	// the child's spawn contract, exported so tests assert the budget DETERMINISTICALLY
    41	// (a wall-clock window cannot prove the 2000ms figure)
    42	const CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS = Object.freeze({ timeout: 2000, killSignal: 'SIGKILL', shell: false });
    43	let childRunnerOverride = null;                 // test seam — SR-55 injects failures here
    44	function _setChildRunner(fn) { childRunnerOverride = fn; }
    45	function defaultChildRunner(payload) {
    46	  const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [CHILD_PATH], {
    47	    input: payload, ...CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS,
    48	    encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 8 * 1024 * 1024,
    49	  });
    50	  return { status: r.status, signal: r.signal || null, stdout: r.stdout || '', error: r.error || null };
    51	}
    52	// resolved = resolveIdPattern's success shape → { batch(texts) → {ids:(string|null)[]} | {failure} }
    53	function makeIdMatcher(resolved) {
    54	  if (resolved.origin !== 'config') {
    55	    const re = new RegExp(resolved.source);
    56	    return { batch: (texts) => ({ ids: texts.map((t) => leadId(t, re)) }) };
    57	  }
    58	  return { batch: (texts) => {
    59	    const runner = childRunnerOverride || defaultChildRunner;
    60	    let r;
    61	    try { r = runner(JSON.stringify({ pattern: resolved.source, texts })); }
    62	    catch (e) { return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(e.message || e)}` }; }
    63	    // timeout FIRST: a real spawnSync timeout carries ETIMEDOUT and/or the kill signal —
    64	    // classifying by r.error first would misreport the termination as a spawn error
    65	    if ((r.error && r.error.code === 'ETIMEDOUT') || r.signal === 'SIGKILL')
    66	      return { failure: 'timeout (budget exceeded, child terminated)' };
    67	    if (r.error) return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(r.error.message || r.error)}` };
    68	    if (r.signal) return { failure: `signal ${r.signal}` };
    69	    if (r.status !== 0) return { failure: `non-zero-exit (${r.status})` };
    70	    let parsed;
    71	    try { parsed = JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { return { failure: 'malformed-output (not JSON)' }; }
    72	    // strict success shape: exactly one JSON document whose ONLY key is a well-formed ids array
    73	    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed) ||
    74	        Object.keys(parsed).length !== 1 || !Array.isArray(parsed.ids) ||
    75	        parsed.ids.length !== texts.length ||
    76	        parsed.ids.some((x) => x !== null && typeof x !== 'string'))
    77	      return { failure: 'malformed-output (bad shape)' };
    78	    return { ids: parsed.ids };
    79	  } };
    80	}
    81	// the one sanitized failure text every consumer surfaces for a config-origin matcher failure
    82	function matcherFailureMsg(failure) {
    83	  return sanitizeMsg(`process-config id-pattern matching failed (${failure})`);
    84	}
    85	const inlineMatcher = (idRe) => makeIdMatcher({ source: idRe.source, origin: 'default' });
    86	
    87	function mdFiles(target) {
    88	  if (!fs.existsSync(target)) return [];
    89	  const st = fs.statSync(target);
    90	  if (st.isFile()) return [target];
    91	  const out = [];
    92	  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(target, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    93	    const p = path.join(target, e.name);
    94	    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...mdFiles(p));
    95	    else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
    96	  }
    97	  return out.sort();
    98	}
    99	
   100	// Shared scenario scan over one text — STRUCTURE ONLY (no ID extraction; that is the
   101	// matcher's job so config-origin patterns stay inside the terminable channel).
   102	// Fenced content is documentation; scenarios inside DEPRECATED requirement blocks
   103	// (the marker REMOVED injects) are excluded in every form — they are neither demanded
   104	// nor listed, and their lingering tests become ORPHAN.
   105	function scanTitles(text, label, pairs) {
   106	  const clean = am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
   107	  let m;
   108	  SCENARIO_RE.lastIndex = 0;
   109	  while ((m = SCENARIO_RE.exec(clean)) !== null) pairs.push([label, m[1]]);
   110	}
   111	
   112	// Batch-bind (label,title) pairs through a matcher → acc, or {failure} when the matcher fails.
   113	function bindPairs(pairs, matcher) {
   114	  const res = matcher.batch(pairs.map(([, title]) => title));
   115	  if (res.failure) return { failure: res.failure };
   116	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
   117	  pairs.forEach(([label, title], i) => {
   118	    const id = res.ids[i];
   119	    if (id) {
   120	      if (!acc.byId.has(id)) { acc.byId.set(id, []); acc.idFiles.set(id, []); }
   121	      acc.byId.get(id).push(title);
   122	      acc.idFiles.get(id).push(label);
   123	    } else acc.unidentified.push([label, title]);
   124	  });
   125	  return acc;
   126	}
   127	
   128	function dupsOf(acc) {
   129	  return [...acc.byId.entries()].filter(([, titles]) => titles.length > 1)
   130	    .map(([id]) => ({ id, files: [...new Set(acc.idFiles.get(id))] }));
   131	}
   132	
   133	// Collect scenarios from spec files.
   134	// → { byId, unidentified:[[file,title]], files, missingTargets:[], duplicates:[{id,files}] }
   135	// (+ failure when the matcher's channel fails — callers fail closed on it)
   136	function collectScenarios(specTargets, idRe, matcher) {
   137	  const m = matcher || inlineMatcher(idRe);
   138	  const missingTargets = specTargets.filter((t) => !fs.existsSync(t));
   139	  const files = [...new Set(specTargets.flatMap(mdFiles))].sort();
   140	  const pairs = [];
   141	  for (const f of files) scanTitles(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'), f, pairs);
   142	  const acc = bindPairs(pairs, m);
   143	  if (acc.failure) return { failure: acc.failure, byId: new Map(), unidentified: [], files, missingTargets, duplicates: [] };
   144	  return { byId: acc.byId, unidentified: acc.unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates: dupsOf(acc) };
   145	}
   146	
   147	// Same collection over in-memory texts (the projection path) — label = store-relative suffix.
   148	function collectScenariosFromTexts(texts, idRe, matcher) {
   149	  const m = matcher || inlineMatcher(idRe);
   150	  const pairs = [];
   151	  for (const [label, text] of texts) scanTitles(text, label, pairs);
   152	  const acc = bindPairs(pairs, m);
   153	  if (acc.failure) return { failure: acc.failure, byId: new Map(), unidentified: [], files: [...texts.keys()], missingTargets: [], duplicates: [] };
   154	  return { byId: acc.byId, unidentified: acc.unidentified, files: [...texts.keys()], missingTargets: [], duplicates: dupsOf(acc) };
   155	}
   156	
   157	// ---- TAP lexer (tap-contract): TAP is a version-aware protocol, not a pile of regexes ----
   158	const PLAN_LINE_RE = /^1\.\.(\d+)\s*(?:#.*)?$/;
   159	const POINT_LINE_RE = /^(?:ok|not ok)(?:\s|$)/;
   160	const POINT_NUM_RE = /^(?:ok|not ok)\s+(\d+)(?:\s|$)/;   // complete numeric token only — `ok 1abc` stays unnumbered
   161	const SUPPORTED_TAP_VERSIONS = new Set([12, 13, 14]);
   162	
   163	// split at the first UNESCAPED '#' (backslash escapes the next char); decode \\ and \# in the
   164	// description ONLY (other backslash pairs stay literal) — so \# is never a directive delimiter
   165	function splitDirective(rest) {
   166	  let desc = '', directive = null;
   167	  for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i++) {
   168	    const c = rest[i];
   169	    if (c === '\\' && i + 1 < rest.length) { desc += c + rest[i + 1]; i++; continue; }
   170	    if (c === '#') { directive = rest.slice(i + 1); break; }
   171	    desc += c;
   172	  }
   173	  const isSkipTodo = directive !== null && /^\s*(skip|todo)/i.test(directive);
   174	  const decoded = desc.replace(/\\([\\#])/g, '$1').trim();
   175	  return { desc: decoded, isSkipTodo };
   176	}
   177	
   178	// does a text carry TAP-shaped tokens? (misrouted-channel detection — bail-out included)
   179	function looksLikeTap(text) {
   180	  return /^(?:TAP version |(?:not )?ok\b|1\.\.\d)/m.test(text || '') || /^\s*bail out!/im.test(text || '');
   181	}
   182	
   183	// Lex normalized stdout into typed top-level tokens. Line endings normalize first
   184	// (CRLF and lone-CR both end a line — a failure never hides behind a line-ending style).
   185	function lexTap(stdout) {
   186	  const lines = stdout.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n');
   187	  const tokens = [], problems = [];
   188	  let yamlOpen = -1;
   189	  for (let idx = 0; idx < lines.length; idx++) {
   190	    const line = lines[idx], n = idx + 1;
   191	    if (yamlOpen >= 0) {                                   // inside a top-level YAML block: opaque
   192	      if (line === '...') yamlOpen = -1;
   193	      continue;
   194	    }
   195	    if (/^\s*bail out!/i.test(line) && line.trim()) {      // case-insensitive, indentation-blind
   196	      tokens.push({ kind: 'bailout', line: line.trim(), n }); continue;
   197	    }
   198	    if (/^\s/.test(line)) continue;                        // indented = nested subtest detail
   199	    if (line === '---') { yamlOpen = n; continue; }
   200	    if (line.startsWith('TAP version ')) {
   201	      const m = /^TAP version (\d+)$/.exec(line);
   202	      tokens.push({ kind: 'version', num: m ? Number(m[1]) : null, raw: line, n }); continue;
   203	    }
   204	    let m;
   205	    if ((m = PLAN_LINE_RE.exec(line))) { tokens.push({ kind: 'plan', total: Number(m[1]), n }); continue; }
   206	    if (/^pragma [+-]\w+$/.test(line)) continue;           // the ONLY ignored TAP-14 construct
   207	    if (POINT_LINE_RE.test(line)) {
   208	      const fail = line.startsWith('not ok');
   209	      const nm = POINT_NUM_RE.exec(line);
   210	      let rest = line.slice(fail ? 'not ok'.length : 'ok'.length);
   211	      if (nm) rest = rest.replace(/^\s+\d+/, '');
   212	      rest = rest.replace(/^\s+/, '');
   213	      const { desc, isSkipTodo } = splitDirective(rest);
   214	      const description = desc.replace(/^-\s+/, '');       // the dash separator is optional (TAP)
   215	      tokens.push({ kind: 'point', fail, num: nm ? Number(nm[1]) : null, description, isSkipTodo, line, n });
   216	      continue;
   217	    }
   218	    // anything else at column 0 is diagnostics (comments, prose) — including `ok:`-style prefixes
   219	  }
   220	  if (yamlOpen >= 0) problems.push(`unterminated YAML diagnostic block opened at line ${yamlOpen} — a stray --- must not swallow the rest of the stream`);
   221	  return { tokens, problems };
   222	}
   223	
   224	// Parse TAP stdout → { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   225	//                      plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems }
   226	// (+ matchFailure when the matcher's channel fails — callers fail closed on it)
   227	// A # SKIP / # TODO directive is neither pass nor fail — a skipped test proves nothing.
   228	function parseTap(out, idRe, matcher) {
   229	  const results = new Map();
   230	  const untagged = [];
   231	  const unattributedFailures = [];
   232	  const { tokens, problems } = lexTap(out);
   233	  const tapProblems = [...problems];
   234	
   235	  // version discipline: at most one, leading, exact digits, inside the closed matrix
   236	  const versions = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'version');
   237	  const firstBody = tokens.find((t) => t.kind !== 'version');
   238	  if (versions.length > 1) tapProblems.push('multiple TAP version lines — one version promise per stream');
   239	  for (const v of versions) {
   240	    if (v.num === null) { tapProblems.push(`unparsable TAP version line at line ${v.n}: ${v.raw}`); continue; }
   241	    if (!SUPPORTED_TAP_VERSIONS.has(v.num)) tapProblems.push(`TAP version ${v.num} is not supported (supported: 12, 13, 14, or no version line)`);
   242	    if (firstBody && v.n > firstBody.n) tapProblems.push(`TAP version line at line ${v.n} arrives after the stream began — the version must lead`);
   243	  }
   244	
   245	  const bail = tokens.find((t) => t.kind === 'bailout');
   246	  const bailout = bail ? bail.line : null;
   247	
   248	  const pts = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'point');
   249	  const plans = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'plan');
   250	  if (plans.length === 1) {
   251	    const plan = plans[0];
   252	    if (pts.some((p) => p.n < plan.n) && pts.some((p) => p.n > plan.n))
   253	      tapProblems.push(`TAP plan at line ${plan.n} sits mid-stream (points before and after) — a plan promises the whole stream`);
   254	    for (const p of pts) if (p.num !== null && (p.num < 1 || p.num > plan.total))
   255	      tapProblems.push(`test point number ${p.num} is outside the plan range 1..${plan.total}`);
   256	  }
   257	  const numSeen = new Set(), dupSet = new Set();
   258	  for (const p of pts) if (p.num !== null) { if (numSeen.has(p.num)) dupSet.add(p.num); else numSeen.add(p.num); }
   259	  const dupNumbers = [...dupSet].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   260	
   261	  const m = matcher || inlineMatcher(idRe);
   262	  const batch = m.batch(pts.map((p) => p.description));
   263	  if (batch.failure) {
   264	    return { results, untagged, untaggedFails: 0, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   265	      plans: plans.map((p) => p.total), points: pts.length, dupNumbers, tapProblems,
   266	      matchFailure: batch.failure };
   267	  }
   268	  pts.forEach((p, i) => {
   269	    const id = batch.ids[i];
   270	    if (id !== null) {
   271	      const cur = results.get(id) || { pass: 0, fail: 0, skip: 0 };
   272	      if (p.isSkipTodo) cur.skip++;
   273	      else cur[p.fail ? 'fail' : 'pass']++;
   274	      results.set(id, cur);
   275	    } else if (p.fail && !p.isSkipTodo) {
   276	      unattributedFailures.push(p.line);
   277	    } else {
   278	      untagged.push(p.description);
   279	    }
   280	  });
   281	  const untaggedFails = unattributedFailures.length;        // kept exported and compatible — doctor consumes it
   282	  return { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   283	    plans: plans.map((p) => p.total), points: pts.length, dupNumbers, tapProblems };
   284	}
   285	
   286	// true when the test command produced output but not a single TAP result line was parsed —
   287	// almost always means the runner used a human reporter (node: add --test-reporter=tap)
   288	function zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures = []) {
   289	  if (results.size > 0 || untagged.length > 0 || unattributedFailures.length > 0) return false;
   290	  if (!out.trim()) return false;
   291	  // a TAP version line or plan (1..N) proves the reporter IS TAP — an empty suite is not a reporter problem
   292	  if (/^TAP version \d+|^1\.\.\d+/m.test(out)) return false;
   293	  return true;
   294	}
   295	
   296	function runTestCommand(cmd, cwd) {
   297	  const r = spawnSync(cmd, { shell: true, cwd, encoding: 'utf8' });
   298	  return {
   299	    out: r.stdout || '',            // TAP parses from stdout ONLY (tap-contract)
   300	    stderr: r.stderr || '',         // a separate diagnostics channel, reported verbatim
   301	    status: r.status,               // null when killed by signal
   302	    signal: r.signal || null,
   303	    error: r.error ? String(r.error.message || r.error) : null,
   304	  };
   305	}
   306	
   307	// Pure evaluation: cross-reference scenarios vs results → verdict object.
   308	// A scenario whose only results are skips is UNBOUND — skips prove nothing.
   309	function evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures = []) {
   310	  const specIds = [...byId.keys()].sort();
   311	  const boundGreen = [], boundRed = [], unbound = [], orphan = [];
   312	  for (const id of specIds) {
   313	    const r = results.get(id);
   314	    if (!r || (r.pass === 0 && r.fail === 0)) unbound.push(id);
   315	    else if (r.fail === 0) boundGreen.push(id);
   316	    else boundRed.push(id);
   317	  }
   318	  for (const id of [...results.keys()].sort()) if (!byId.has(id)) orphan.push(id);
   319	  const unattributed = unattributedFailures;
   320	  const clean = !boundRed.length && !unbound.length && !orphan.length && !unidentified.length && !unattributed.length;
   321	  return { boundGreen, boundRed, unbound, orphan, unidentified, unattributed, clean };
   322	}
   323	
   324	// Fail-closed guard: reasons this run cannot be trusted regardless of the TAP picture.
   325	// Returns [] when the run is trustworthy.
   326	function infraErrors(run) {
   327	  const errs = [];
   328	  for (const t of run.missingTargets) errs.push(`spec target does not exist: ${t}`);
   329	  if (!run.missingTargets.length && run.fileCount === 0)
   330	    errs.push('no spec files found under the given targets — nothing to verify');
   331	  if (run.fileCount > 0 && run.scenarioCount === 0)
   332	    errs.push('spec files contain zero scenarios — nothing to verify');
   333	  if (run.exec.error) errs.push(`test command failed to spawn: ${run.exec.error}`);
   334	  if (run.exec.signal) errs.push(`test command was killed by signal ${run.exec.signal}`);
   335	  if (run.bailout) errs.push(`test run aborted: ${run.bailout}`);
   336	  for (const p of run.tapProblems || []) errs.push(`TAP protocol: ${p}`);
   337	  if (run.noTap)
   338	    errs.push('test command produced output but ZERO TAP results were parsed on stdout — the command is probably not emitting TAP (node test runner: add --test-reporter=tap; TAP on stderr: append 2>&1)');
   339	  // the TAP plan is a checked promise — defensive defaults keep legacy run shapes
   340	  // (direct callers of this exported helper) valid; only verify-created runs carry the fields
   341	  const plans = run.plans || [], points = run.points || 0, dupNumbers = run.dupNumbers || [];
   342	  if (plans.length > 1)
   343	    errs.push(`multiple TAP plans in one run (${plans.map((n) => `1..${n}`).join(', ')}) — cannot attribute test points to plans; run one TAP stream per verify (split the test command)`);
   344	  else if (plans.length === 1 && plans[0] !== points)
   345	    errs.push(`TAP plan declares ${plans[0]} test point(s) but ${points} were parsed — output truncated or garbled; refusing to trust this run`);
   346	  else if (plans.length === 1 && dupNumbers.length)
   347	    errs.push(`duplicate TAP test-point number(s): ${dupNumbers.join(', ')}`);
   348	  if (run.exec.status !== null && run.exec.status !== 0 && run.failCount === 0)
   349	    errs.push(`test command exited with status ${run.exec.status} but no parsed TAP failure explains it — the TAP picture is incomplete or truncated; refusing to trust this run`);
   350	  return errs;
   351	}
   352	
   353	function formatReport(v, results, fileCount, extras = {}) {
   354	  const lines = [];
   355	  const specCount = v.boundGreen.length + v.boundRed.length + v.unbound.length;
   356	  const tagged = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0);
   357	  const skipped = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + (r.skip || 0), 0);
   358	  lines.push(`specs: ${fileCount} file(s), ${specCount} identified scenario(s)`);
   359	  lines.push(`tests: ${tagged} tagged result(s)${skipped ? `, ${skipped} skipped (not counted)` : ''}\n`);
   360	  const groups = [['BOUND-GREEN', v.boundGreen], ['BOUND-RED', v.boundRed],
   361	                 ['UNBOUND (scenario, no test)', v.unbound], ['ORPHAN (test, no scenario)', v.orphan]];
   362	  for (const [label, items] of groups) {
   363	    if (!items.length) continue;
   364	    lines.push(`${label === 'BOUND-GREEN' ? '✓' : '✗'} ${label}: ${items.length}`);
   365	    for (const id of items) {
   366	      const r = results.get(id);
   367	      lines.push(`    ${id}${r ? `  ${r.pass}p/${r.fail}f${r.skip ? `/${r.skip}s` : ''}` : ''}`);
   368	    }
   369	  }
   370	  if (v.unattributed && v.unattributed.length) {
   371	    lines.push(`✗ UNATTRIBUTED FAILURES (not ok without a scenario ID): ${v.unattributed.length}`);
   372	    for (const raw of v.unattributed.slice(0, 20))
   373	      lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   374	    if (v.unattributed.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${v.unattributed.length - 20} more`);
   375	  }
   376	  if (v.unidentified.length) {
   377	    lines.push(`✗ UNIDENTIFIED (scenario, no ID — unbindable): ${v.unidentified.length}`);
   378	    for (const [f, t] of v.unidentified) lines.push(`    ${path.basename(f)}: ${t.slice(0, 60)}`);
   379	  }
   380	  if (extras.duplicates && extras.duplicates.length) {
   381	    lines.push(`✗ DUPLICATE scenario IDs (ambiguous binding): ${extras.duplicates.length}`);
   382	    for (const d of extras.duplicates) lines.push(`    ${d.id}: ${d.files.map((f) => path.basename(f)).join(', ')}`);
   383	  }
   384	  if (extras.stderr && extras.stderr.trim()) {
   385	    const sl = extras.stderr.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n').filter((l) => l.length);
   386	    lines.push(`— STDERR DIAGNOSTICS (not TAP): ${sl.length} line(s)`);
   387	    for (const raw of sl.slice(0, 20)) lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   388	    if (sl.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${sl.length - 20} more`);
   389	  }
   390	  const errs = extras.errors || [];
   391	  lines.push('\nRESULT: ' + (errs.length ? 'ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)'
   392	    : (v.clean && !(extras.duplicates || []).length ? 'GREEN — spec is the test suite' : 'GAPS')));
   393	  return lines.join('\n');
   394	}
   395	
   396	// Projection inputs for `verify --change` (all failure classes → run.errors, exit 2).
   397	// Roots resolve against cwd exactly as `apriori status` resolves change dirs.
   398	function buildChangeProjection(change, cwd) {
   399	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   400	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   401	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   402	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   403	  if (d.errors.length) return { projection, errors: d.errors, texts: null };
   404	  const p = am.buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, change);
   405	  projection.modules = p.modules;
   406	  projection.conflicts = p.conflicts;
   407	  projection.unstampedMutations = p.unstampedMutations;      // WARN grade — cli prints, verdict untouched
   408	  projection.notes = p.notes;
   409	  const errors = [...p.validation, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches];
   410	  if (p.conflicts.length) errors.push(`projection has ${p.conflicts.length} merge conflict(s) — no trustworthy projection: ${p.conflicts.join('; ')}`);
   411	  return { projection, errors, texts: errors.length ? null : p.texts };
   412	}
   413	
   414	// an errors-only run shape shared by every pre-test early exit (failed projection,
   415	// invalid pattern, matcher-channel failure) — fail closed WITHOUT running any tests
   416	function errorsRun(errors, projection) {
   417	  const verdict = evaluate(new Map(), [], new Map(), []);
   418	  const run = { verdict, results: new Map(), fileCount: 0, failCount: 0, scenarioCount: 0,
   419	    missingTargets: [], duplicates: [], bailout: null, noTap: false, tapProblems: [], stderr: '',
   420	    exec: { status: null, signal: null, error: null }, errors };
   421	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   422	  return run;
   423	}
   424	
   425	// enumeration-only projection metadata for the invalid-pattern early exit on --change runs:
   426	// SR-23's projection contract survives, yet no delta CONTENT is read (directory listing only)
   427	function enumerateProjection(change, cwd) {
   428	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   429	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   430	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   431	  if (!d.errors.length) projection.modules = d.files.map((f) => f.suffix);
   432	  return projection;
   433	}
   434	
   435	// Full verify run. opts: { specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd }
   436	function verify(opts) {
   437	  const cwd = opts.cwd || '.';
   438	  // pattern resolution FIRST — before any spec content is read or any test command spawned
   439	  const resolved = resolveIdPattern(cwd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   440	  if (resolved.error)
   441	    return errorsRun([resolved.error], opts.change ? enumerateProjection(opts.change, cwd) : null);
   442	  const idRe = new RegExp(resolved.source);
   443	  const matcher = makeIdMatcher(resolved);
   444	  let collected, projection = null;
   445	  if (opts.change) {
   446	    const b = buildChangeProjection(opts.change, cwd);
   447	    projection = b.projection;
   448	    if (!b.texts) return errorsRun(b.errors, projection);
   449	    collected = collectScenariosFromTexts(b.texts, idRe, matcher);
   450	  } else {
   451	    collected = collectScenarios(opts.specs, idRe, matcher);
   452	  }
   453	  if (collected.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure)], projection);
   454	  const { byId, unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates } = collected;
   455	  const exec = runTestCommand(opts.testCmd, cwd);
   456	  const parsed = parseTap(exec.out, idRe, matcher);
   457	  if (parsed.matchFailure) {
   458	    const run = errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(parsed.matchFailure)], projection);
   459	    run.exec = { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error };
   460	    run.stderr = exec.stderr || '';
   461	    return run;
   462	  }
   463	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems } = parsed;
   464	  const verdict = evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures);
   465	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
   466	  const run = {
   467	    verdict, results, fileCount: files.length, failCount,
   468	    scenarioCount: byId.size + unidentified.length,
   469	    missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers,
   470	    noTap: zeroTapParsed(exec.out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures)
   471	      || (!exec.out.trim() && looksLikeTap(exec.stderr)),  // TAP went to stderr — same zero-TAP cure (2>&1)
   472	    tapProblems,
   473	    stderr: exec.stderr || '',
   474	    exec: { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error },
   475	  };
   476	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   477	  run.errors = infraErrors(run);
   478	  return run;
   479	}
   480	
   481	// machine-consumable shape for a verify run
   482	function verifyJson(run) {
   483	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   484	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 && run.errors.length === 0;
   485	  const json = projectionless(run, clean);
   486	  if (run.projection) json.projection = run.projection;   // ONLY --change runs carry this field
   487	  return json;
   488	}
   489	
   490	function projectionless(run, clean) {
   491	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   492	  return {
   493	    clean,
   494	    result: run.errors.length ? 'ERROR' : (clean ? 'GREEN' : 'GAPS'),
   495	    errors: run.errors,
   496	    specFiles: run.fileCount,
   497	    exec: run.exec,
   498	    duplicates: run.duplicates,
   499	    boundGreen: v.boundGreen.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   500	    boundRed: v.boundRed.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   501	    unbound: v.unbound,
   502	    orphan: v.orphan.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   503	    unidentified: v.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })),
   504	    unattributedFailures: { count: (v.unattributed || []).length, lines: v.unattributed || [] },
   505	    stderr: run.stderr || '',
   506	  };
   507	}

   509	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   510	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   511	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   512	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   513	  return value;
   514	}
   515	
   516	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori verify --specs <dir...> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n   or: apriori verify --change <name> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n(--test-cmd may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has a test-cmd row;\n --id-pattern may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has an id-pattern row)';
   517	
   518	function cli(argv) {
   519	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'verify', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   520	    flags: { '--specs': 'multi', '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   521	    const a = { specs: f['--specs'] || [], change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   522	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (SR-52 empty flag)
   523	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   524	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   525	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   526	      return 2;
   527	    }
   528	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   529	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   530	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   531	      a.testCmd = c;
   532	    }
   533	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   534	    const run = verify(a);
   535	    if (run.projection)
   536	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   537	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   538	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   539	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   540	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr }));
   541	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   542	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   543	  });
   544	}

     2	/*
     3	 * apriori gate — aggregate the MECHANICAL exit conditions for one change into one exit code.
     4	 * 0 = PASS · 1 = BLOCKED · 2 = the evaluation itself is untrustworthy.
     5	 * Strictly read-only. PASS covers machine checks only — human gates remain human.

     8	const path = require('path');

    16	const STEP_ENUM = ['STEP0', 'STEP1', 'STEP2', 'STEP3', 'STEP4', 'STEP5', 'STEP6',
    17	  'INTENT-CARD', 'SPIKE', 'EXTRACTION', 'DONE', 'ABANDONED'];

    24	// --- individual checks; each returns {id, status: 'pass'|'blocked'|'n/a', detail} ---

    26	function checkFlowState(state, name) {
    27	  for (const key of ['change', 'tier', 'track', 'lineage', 'current-step']) {
    28	    const v = state[key];
    29	    if (v === undefined || v === '') return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: required key '${key}' missing` };
    30	    if (v.includes('<') || v.includes('>')) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: '${key}' is an unfilled placeholder (${v})` };
    31	  }
    32	  if (state.change !== name) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'change' is '${state.change}', expected '${name}'` };
    33	  if (!STEP_ENUM.includes(state['current-step'])) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'current-step' '${state['current-step']}' not in the legal vocabulary` };
    34	  if (!TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier)) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'tier' '${state.tier}' not in {trivial, medium, large}` };
    35	  return { id: 'C3', status: 'pass', detail: `legal (tier ${state.tier}, ${state['current-step']})` };
    36	}

    38	function checkTasks(dir, tier) {
    39	  const p = path.join(dir, 'tasks.md');
    40	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    41	    return tier === 'trivial'
    42	      ? { id: 'C2', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no tasks.md — trivial tier has no STEP2' }
    43	      : { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md missing at ${p}` };
    44	  }
    45	  const open = (fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8').match(/^\s*-\s\[\s\]/gm) || []).length;
    46	  return open
    47	    ? { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md has ${open} unchecked box(es)` }
    48	    : { id: 'C2', status: 'pass', detail: 'all tasks checked' };
    49	}

    51	// the ledger vocabulary, one classifier shared by C4 and the corpus test (GT-15).
    52	// leading token, case-insensitive; rejected-verified BEFORE rejected (alternation order).
    53	const STATUS_RE = /^(open|fixed|verified|rejected-verified|rejected|advisory-acked|waived)\b/i;
    54	function classifyStatus(status) {
    55	  const m = STATUS_RE.exec(status);
    56	  if (!m) return { legal: false, terminal: false, needsReason: false, hasReason: false, isWaived: false };
    57	  const tok = m[1].toLowerCase();
    58	  const needsReason = tok === 'rejected' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived';
    59	  return {
    60	    legal: true,
    61	    terminal: tok === 'verified' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived' || tok === 'advisory-acked',
    62	    needsReason,
    63	    hasReason: !needsReason || /\w/.test(status.slice(m[0].length)),
    64	    isWaived: tok === 'waived',
    65	    token: tok,
    66	  };
    67	}
    68	
    69	const escapeRe = (s) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    70	
    71	// gates: entries from a flow-state text — the block runs from the unindented `gates:` line
    72	// to the next unindented top-level key; entries start `- `, continuation lines attach.
    73	function gatesEntries(flowText) {
    74	  const start = flowText.search(/^gates:/m);
    75	  if (start < 0) return [];
    76	  const rest = flowText.slice(start + 'gates:'.length);
    77	  const end = rest.search(/^[A-Za-z][\w-]*:/m);
    78	  const block = end < 0 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
    79	  const entries = [];
    80	  for (const line of block.split('\n')) {
    81	    if (/^\s*-\s/.test(line)) entries.push(line.trim());
    82	    else if (entries.length && line.trim()) entries[entries.length - 1] += ' ' + line.trim();
    83	  }
    84	  return entries;
    85	}
    86	
    87	// a waive is a HUMAN act: one single gates: entry must carry the row ID as an exact
    88	// token (LS-1 never matches inside LS-10) AND the word waive/waived.
    89	function waiveEvidence(flowText, id) {
    90	  const idRe = new RegExp(`(^|[^A-Za-z0-9-])${escapeRe(id)}([^A-Za-z0-9-]|$)`);
    91	  return gatesEntries(flowText).some((e) => idRe.test(e) && /waiv/i.test(e));
    92	}
    93	
    94	function checkLedger(tier, stage, dir) {
    95	  const p = path.join(dir, 'review', 'issues.md');
    96	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    97	    return tier === 'trivial'
    98	      ? { id: 'C4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no ledger — trivial tier may never open one' }
    99	      : { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: `ledger missing at ${p}` };
   100	  }
   101	  const rows = parseLedger(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
   102	  const fsPath = dir ? path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md') : null;
   103	  const flowText = fsPath && fs.existsSync(fsPath) ? fs.readFileSync(fsPath, 'utf8') : '';
   104	  const bad = [];
   105	  for (const r of rows) {
   106	    const c = classifyStatus(r.status);
   107	    if (!c.legal) { bad.push(`${r.id}: '${r.status}' is not in the ledger vocabulary`); continue; }
   108	    if (c.needsReason && !c.hasReason) { bad.push(`${r.id} is ${c.token} without a reason`); continue; }
   109	    if (c.isWaived && !waiveEvidence(flowText, r.id)) {
   110	      bad.push(`${r.id} is waived without a gates: entry recording the human decision`); continue;
   111	    }
   112	    if (c.token === 'open') { bad.push(`${r.id} is open`); continue; }
   113	    if (stage === 'archived' && !c.terminal) {
   114	      bad.push(c.token === 'fixed'
   115	        ? `${r.id} is fixed but never verified — reviewer must verify, or the human waives it`
   116	        : `${r.id} is rejected without reviewer concurrence — flip to rejected-verified, or the human waives it`);
   117	    }
   118	  }
   119	  return bad.length
   120	    ? { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: bad.join('; ') }
   121	    : { id: 'C4', status: 'pass', detail: `${rows.length} row(s), none blocking` };
   122	}

   124	// the bundle review/ dir is the shared evidence root for C4 and C5 — when present it must be
   125	// a REAL contained directory (symlinked/escaping/non-dir entries block, never read through)
   126	function reviewDirDefect(dir) {
   127	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   128	  let st;
   129	  try { st = fs.lstatSync(rd); } catch { return null; }  // lstat, not exists: a dangling symlink is a defect, not absence
   130	  if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return `review is a symlink: ${rd}`;
   131	  if (!st.isDirectory()) return `review is not a directory: ${rd}`;
   132	  if (!containsReal(dir, rd)) return `review escapes the change dir: ${rd}`;
   133	  return null;
   134	}
