   157	// ---- TAP lexer (tap-contract): TAP is a version-aware protocol, not a pile of regexes ----
   158	const PLAN_LINE_RE = /^1\.\.(\d+)\s*(?:#.*)?$/;
   159	const POINT_LINE_RE = /^(?:ok|not ok)(?:\s|$)/;
   160	const POINT_NUM_RE = /^(?:ok|not ok)\s+(\d+)(?:\s|$)/;   // complete numeric token only — `ok 1abc` stays unnumbered
   161	const SUPPORTED_TAP_VERSIONS = new Set([12, 13, 14]);
   162	
   163	// split at the first UNESCAPED '#' (backslash escapes the next char); decode \\ and \# in the
   164	// description ONLY (other backslash pairs stay literal) — so \# is never a directive delimiter
   165	function splitDirective(rest) {
   166	  let desc = '', directive = null;
   167	  for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i++) {
   168	    const c = rest[i];
   169	    if (c === '\\' && i + 1 < rest.length) { desc += c + rest[i + 1]; i++; continue; }
   170	    if (c === '#') { directive = rest.slice(i + 1); break; }
   171	    desc += c;
   172	  }
   173	  const isSkipTodo = directive !== null && /^\s*(skip|todo)/i.test(directive);
   174	  const decoded = desc.replace(/\\([\\#])/g, '$1').trim();
   175	  return { desc: decoded, isSkipTodo };
   176	}
   177	
   178	// does a text carry TAP-shaped tokens? (misrouted-channel detection — bail-out included)
   179	function looksLikeTap(text) {
   180	  return /^(?:TAP version |(?:not )?ok\b|1\.\.\d)/m.test(text || '') || /^\s*bail out!/im.test(text || '');
   181	}
   182	
   183	// Lex normalized stdout into typed top-level tokens. Line endings normalize first
   184	// (CRLF and lone-CR both end a line — a failure never hides behind a line-ending style).
   185	function lexTap(stdout) {
   186	  const lines = stdout.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n');
   187	  const tokens = [], problems = [];
   188	  let yamlOpen = -1;
   189	  for (let idx = 0; idx < lines.length; idx++) {
   190	    const line = lines[idx], n = idx + 1;
   191	    if (yamlOpen >= 0) {                                   // inside a top-level YAML block: opaque
   192	      if (line === '...') yamlOpen = -1;
   193	      continue;
   194	    }
   195	    if (/^\s*bail out!/i.test(line) && line.trim()) {      // case-insensitive, indentation-blind
   196	      tokens.push({ kind: 'bailout', line: line.trim(), n }); continue;
   197	    }
   198	    if (/^\s/.test(line)) continue;                        // indented = nested subtest detail
   199	    if (line === '---') { yamlOpen = n; continue; }
   200	    if (line.startsWith('TAP version ')) {
   201	      const m = /^TAP version (\d+)$/.exec(line);
   202	      tokens.push({ kind: 'version', num: m ? Number(m[1]) : null, raw: line, n }); continue;
   203	    }
   204	    let m;
   205	    if ((m = PLAN_LINE_RE.exec(line))) { tokens.push({ kind: 'plan', total: Number(m[1]), n }); continue; }
   206	    if (/^pragma [+-]\w+$/.test(line)) continue;           // the ONLY ignored TAP-14 construct
   207	    if (POINT_LINE_RE.test(line)) {
   208	      const fail = line.startsWith('not ok');
   209	      const nm = POINT_NUM_RE.exec(line);
   210	      let rest = line.slice(fail ? 'not ok'.length : 'ok'.length);
   211	      if (nm) rest = rest.replace(/^\s+\d+/, '');
   212	      rest = rest.replace(/^\s+/, '');
   213	      const { desc, isSkipTodo } = splitDirective(rest);
   214	      const description = desc.replace(/^-\s+/, '');       // the dash separator is optional (TAP)
   215	      tokens.push({ kind: 'point', fail, num: nm ? Number(nm[1]) : null, description, isSkipTodo, line, n });
   216	      continue;
   217	    }
   218	    // anything else at column 0 is diagnostics (comments, prose) — including `ok:`-style prefixes
   219	  }
   220	  if (yamlOpen >= 0) problems.push(`unterminated YAML diagnostic block opened at line ${yamlOpen} — a stray --- must not swallow the rest of the stream`);
   221	  return { tokens, problems };
   222	}
   223	
   224	// Parse TAP stdout → { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   225	//                      plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems }
   226	// (+ matchFailure when the matcher's channel fails — callers fail closed on it)
   227	// A # SKIP / # TODO directive is neither pass nor fail — a skipped test proves nothing.
   228	function parseTap(out, idRe, matcher) {
   229	  const results = new Map();
   230	  const untagged = [];
   231	  const unattributedFailures = [];
   232	  const { tokens, problems } = lexTap(out);
   233	  const tapProblems = [...problems];
   234	
   235	  // version discipline: at most one, leading, exact digits, inside the closed matrix
   236	  const versions = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'version');
   237	  const firstBody = tokens.find((t) => t.kind !== 'version');
   238	  if (versions.length > 1) tapProblems.push('multiple TAP version lines — one version promise per stream');
   239	  for (const v of versions) {
   240	    if (v.num === null) { tapProblems.push(`unparsable TAP version line at line ${v.n}: ${v.raw}`); continue; }
   241	    if (!SUPPORTED_TAP_VERSIONS.has(v.num)) tapProblems.push(`TAP version ${v.num} is not supported (supported: 12, 13, 14, or no version line)`);
   242	    if (firstBody && v.n > firstBody.n) tapProblems.push(`TAP version line at line ${v.n} arrives after the stream began — the version must lead`);
   243	  }
   244	
   245	  const bail = tokens.find((t) => t.kind === 'bailout');
   246	  const bailout = bail ? bail.line : null;
   247	
   248	  const pts = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'point');
   249	  const plans = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'plan');
   250	  if (plans.length === 1) {
   251	    const plan = plans[0];
   252	    if (pts.some((p) => p.n < plan.n) && pts.some((p) => p.n > plan.n))
   253	      tapProblems.push(`TAP plan at line ${plan.n} sits mid-stream (points before and after) — a plan promises the whole stream`);
   254	    for (const p of pts) if (p.num !== null && (p.num < 1 || p.num > plan.total))
   255	      tapProblems.push(`test point number ${p.num} is outside the plan range 1..${plan.total}`);
   256	  }
   257	  const numSeen = new Set(), dupSet = new Set();
   258	  for (const p of pts) if (p.num !== null) { if (numSeen.has(p.num)) dupSet.add(p.num); else numSeen.add(p.num); }
   259	  const dupNumbers = [...dupSet].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   260	
   261	  const m = matcher || inlineMatcher(idRe);
   262	  const batch = m.batch(pts.map((p) => p.description));
   263	  if (batch.failure) {
   264	    return { results, untagged, untaggedFails: 0, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   265	      plans: plans.map((p) => p.total), points: pts.length, dupNumbers, tapProblems,
   266	      matchFailure: batch.failure };
   267	  }
   268	  pts.forEach((p, i) => {
   269	    const id = batch.ids[i];
   270	    if (id !== null) {
   271	      const cur = results.get(id) || { pass: 0, fail: 0, skip: 0 };
   272	      if (p.isSkipTodo) cur.skip++;
   273	      else cur[p.fail ? 'fail' : 'pass']++;
   274	      results.set(id, cur);
   275	    } else if (p.fail && !p.isSkipTodo) {
   276	      unattributedFailures.push(p.line);
   277	    } else {
   278	      untagged.push(p.description);
   279	    }
   280	  });
   281	  const untaggedFails = unattributedFailures.length;        // kept exported and compatible — doctor consumes it
   282	  return { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   283	    plans: plans.map((p) => p.total), points: pts.length, dupNumbers, tapProblems };
   284	}
   285	
   286	// true when the test command produced output but not a single TAP result line was parsed —
   287	// almost always means the runner used a human reporter (node: add --test-reporter=tap)
   288	function zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures = []) {
   289	  if (results.size > 0 || untagged.length > 0 || unattributedFailures.length > 0) return false;
   290	  if (!out.trim()) return false;
   291	  // a TAP version line or plan (1..N) proves the reporter IS TAP — an empty suite is not a reporter problem
   292	  if (/^TAP version \d+|^1\.\.\d+/m.test(out)) return false;
   293	  return true;
   294	}
   295	
   296	function runTestCommand(cmd, cwd) {
   297	  const r = spawnSync(cmd, { shell: true, cwd, encoding: 'utf8' });
   298	  return {
   299	    out: r.stdout || '',            // TAP parses from stdout ONLY (tap-contract)
   300	    stderr: r.stderr || '',         // a separate diagnostics channel, reported verbatim
   301	    status: r.status,               // null when killed by signal
   302	    signal: r.signal || null,
   303	    error: r.error ? String(r.error.message || r.error) : null,
   304	  };
   305	}
   306	
   307	// Pure evaluation: cross-reference scenarios vs results → verdict object.
   308	// A scenario whose only results are skips is UNBOUND — skips prove nothing.
   309	function evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures = []) {
   310	  const specIds = [...byId.keys()].sort();
   311	  const boundGreen = [], boundRed = [], unbound = [], orphan = [];
   312	  for (const id of specIds) {
   313	    const r = results.get(id);
   314	    if (!r || (r.pass === 0 && r.fail === 0)) unbound.push(id);
   315	    else if (r.fail === 0) boundGreen.push(id);
   316	    else boundRed.push(id);
   317	  }
   318	  for (const id of [...results.keys()].sort()) if (!byId.has(id)) orphan.push(id);
   319	  const unattributed = unattributedFailures;
   320	  const clean = !boundRed.length && !unbound.length && !orphan.length && !unidentified.length && !unattributed.length;
   321	  return { boundGreen, boundRed, unbound, orphan, unidentified, unattributed, clean };
   322	}
   323	
   324	// Fail-closed guard: reasons this run cannot be trusted regardless of the TAP picture.
   325	// Returns [] when the run is trustworthy.
   326	function infraErrors(run) {
   327	  const errs = [];
   328	  for (const t of run.missingTargets) errs.push(`spec target does not exist: ${t}`);
   329	  if (!run.missingTargets.length && run.fileCount === 0)
   330	    errs.push('no spec files found under the given targets — nothing to verify');
   331	  if (run.fileCount > 0 && run.scenarioCount === 0)
   332	    errs.push('spec files contain zero scenarios — nothing to verify');
   333	  if (run.exec.error) errs.push(`test command failed to spawn: ${run.exec.error}`);
   334	  if (run.exec.signal) errs.push(`test command was killed by signal ${run.exec.signal}`);
   335	  if (run.bailout) errs.push(`test run aborted: ${run.bailout}`);
   336	  for (const p of run.tapProblems || []) errs.push(`TAP protocol: ${p}`);
   337	  if (run.noTap)
   338	    errs.push('test command produced output but ZERO TAP results were parsed on stdout — the command is probably not emitting TAP (node test runner: add --test-reporter=tap; TAP on stderr: append 2>&1)');
   339	  // the TAP plan is a checked promise — defensive defaults keep legacy run shapes
   340	  // (direct callers of this exported helper) valid; only verify-created runs carry the fields
   341	  const plans = run.plans || [], points = run.points || 0, dupNumbers = run.dupNumbers || [];
   342	  if (plans.length > 1)
   343	    errs.push(`multiple TAP plans in one run (${plans.map((n) => `1..${n}`).join(', ')}) — cannot attribute test points to plans; run one TAP stream per verify (split the test command)`);
   344	  else if (plans.length === 1 && plans[0] !== points)
   345	    errs.push(`TAP plan declares ${plans[0]} test point(s) but ${points} were parsed — output truncated or garbled; refusing to trust this run`);
   346	  else if (plans.length === 1 && dupNumbers.length)
   347	    errs.push(`duplicate TAP test-point number(s): ${dupNumbers.join(', ')}`);
   348	  if (run.exec.status !== null && run.exec.status !== 0 && run.failCount === 0)
   349	    errs.push(`test command exited with status ${run.exec.status} but no parsed TAP failure explains it — the TAP picture is incomplete or truncated; refusing to trust this run`);
   350	  return errs;
   351	}
   352	
   353	function formatReport(v, results, fileCount, extras = {}) {
   354	  const lines = [];
   355	  const specCount = v.boundGreen.length + v.boundRed.length + v.unbound.length;
   356	  const tagged = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0);
   357	  const skipped = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + (r.skip || 0), 0);
   358	  lines.push(`specs: ${fileCount} file(s), ${specCount} identified scenario(s)`);
   359	  lines.push(`tests: ${tagged} tagged result(s)${skipped ? `, ${skipped} skipped (not counted)` : ''}\n`);
   360	  const groups = [['BOUND-GREEN', v.boundGreen], ['BOUND-RED', v.boundRed],

exec
/usr/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba lib/spec-runner.js | sed -n '350,590p'" in /mnt/d/Workbench/misc/apriori-spec-development
 succeeded in 95ms:
   350	  return errs;
   351	}
   352	
   353	function formatReport(v, results, fileCount, extras = {}) {
   354	  const lines = [];
   355	  const specCount = v.boundGreen.length + v.boundRed.length + v.unbound.length;
   356	  const tagged = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0);
   357	  const skipped = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + (r.skip || 0), 0);
   358	  lines.push(`specs: ${fileCount} file(s), ${specCount} identified scenario(s)`);
   359	  lines.push(`tests: ${tagged} tagged result(s)${skipped ? `, ${skipped} skipped (not counted)` : ''}\n`);
   360	  const groups = [['BOUND-GREEN', v.boundGreen], ['BOUND-RED', v.boundRed],
   361	                 ['UNBOUND (scenario, no test)', v.unbound], ['ORPHAN (test, no scenario)', v.orphan]];
   362	  for (const [label, items] of groups) {
   363	    if (!items.length) continue;
   364	    lines.push(`${label === 'BOUND-GREEN' ? '✓' : '✗'} ${label}: ${items.length}`);
   365	    for (const id of items) {
   366	      const r = results.get(id);
   367	      lines.push(`    ${id}${r ? `  ${r.pass}p/${r.fail}f${r.skip ? `/${r.skip}s` : ''}` : ''}`);
   368	    }
   369	  }
   370	  if (v.unattributed && v.unattributed.length) {
   371	    lines.push(`✗ UNATTRIBUTED FAILURES (not ok without a scenario ID): ${v.unattributed.length}`);
   372	    for (const raw of v.unattributed.slice(0, 20))
   373	      lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   374	    if (v.unattributed.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${v.unattributed.length - 20} more`);
   375	  }
   376	  if (v.unidentified.length) {
   377	    lines.push(`✗ UNIDENTIFIED (scenario, no ID — unbindable): ${v.unidentified.length}`);
   378	    for (const [f, t] of v.unidentified) lines.push(`    ${path.basename(f)}: ${t.slice(0, 60)}`);
   379	  }
   380	  if (extras.duplicates && extras.duplicates.length) {
   381	    lines.push(`✗ DUPLICATE scenario IDs (ambiguous binding): ${extras.duplicates.length}`);
   382	    for (const d of extras.duplicates) lines.push(`    ${d.id}: ${d.files.map((f) => path.basename(f)).join(', ')}`);
   383	  }
   384	  if (extras.stderr && extras.stderr.trim()) {
   385	    const sl = extras.stderr.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n').filter((l) => l.length);
   386	    lines.push(`— STDERR DIAGNOSTICS (not TAP): ${sl.length} line(s)`);
   387	    for (const raw of sl.slice(0, 20)) lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   388	    if (sl.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${sl.length - 20} more`);
   389	  }
   390	  const errs = extras.errors || [];
   391	  lines.push('\nRESULT: ' + (errs.length ? 'ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)'
   392	    : (v.clean && !(extras.duplicates || []).length ? 'GREEN — spec is the test suite' : 'GAPS')));
   393	  return lines.join('\n');
   394	}
   395	
   396	// Projection inputs for `verify --change` (all failure classes → run.errors, exit 2).
   397	// Roots resolve against cwd exactly as `apriori status` resolves change dirs.
   398	function buildChangeProjection(change, cwd) {
   399	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   400	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   401	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   402	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   403	  if (d.errors.length) return { projection, errors: d.errors, texts: null };
   404	  const p = am.buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, change);
   405	  projection.modules = p.modules;
   406	  projection.conflicts = p.conflicts;
   407	  projection.unstampedMutations = p.unstampedMutations;      // WARN grade — cli prints, verdict untouched
   408	  projection.notes = p.notes;
   409	  const errors = [...p.validation, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches];
   410	  if (p.conflicts.length) errors.push(`projection has ${p.conflicts.length} merge conflict(s) — no trustworthy projection: ${p.conflicts.join('; ')}`);
   411	  return { projection, errors, texts: errors.length ? null : p.texts };
   412	}
   413	
   414	// an errors-only run shape shared by every pre-test early exit (failed projection,
   415	// invalid pattern, matcher-channel failure) — fail closed WITHOUT running any tests
   416	function errorsRun(errors, projection) {
   417	  const verdict = evaluate(new Map(), [], new Map(), []);
   418	  const run = { verdict, results: new Map(), fileCount: 0, failCount: 0, scenarioCount: 0,
   419	    missingTargets: [], duplicates: [], bailout: null, noTap: false, tapProblems: [], stderr: '',
   420	    exec: { status: null, signal: null, error: null }, errors };
   421	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   422	  return run;
   423	}
   424	
   425	// enumeration-only projection metadata for the invalid-pattern early exit on --change runs:
   426	// SR-23's projection contract survives, yet no delta CONTENT is read (directory listing only)
   427	function enumerateProjection(change, cwd) {
   428	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   429	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   430	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   431	  if (!d.errors.length) projection.modules = d.files.map((f) => f.suffix);
   432	  return projection;
   433	}
   434	
   435	// Full verify run. opts: { specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd }
   436	function verify(opts) {
   437	  const cwd = opts.cwd || '.';
   438	  // pattern resolution FIRST — before any spec content is read or any test command spawned
   439	  const resolved = resolveIdPattern(cwd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   440	  if (resolved.error)
   441	    return errorsRun([resolved.error], opts.change ? enumerateProjection(opts.change, cwd) : null);
   442	  const idRe = new RegExp(resolved.source);
   443	  const matcher = makeIdMatcher(resolved);
   444	  let collected, projection = null;
   445	  if (opts.change) {
   446	    const b = buildChangeProjection(opts.change, cwd);
   447	    projection = b.projection;
   448	    if (!b.texts) return errorsRun(b.errors, projection);
   449	    collected = collectScenariosFromTexts(b.texts, idRe, matcher);
   450	  } else {
   451	    collected = collectScenarios(opts.specs, idRe, matcher);
   452	  }
   453	  if (collected.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure)], projection);
   454	  const { byId, unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates } = collected;
   455	  const exec = runTestCommand(opts.testCmd, cwd);
   456	  const parsed = parseTap(exec.out, idRe, matcher);
   457	  if (parsed.matchFailure) {
   458	    const run = errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(parsed.matchFailure)], projection);
   459	    run.exec = { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error };
   460	    run.stderr = exec.stderr || '';
   461	    return run;
   462	  }
   463	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems } = parsed;
   464	  const verdict = evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures);
   465	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
   466	  const run = {
   467	    verdict, results, fileCount: files.length, failCount,
   468	    scenarioCount: byId.size + unidentified.length,
   469	    missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers,
   470	    noTap: zeroTapParsed(exec.out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures)
   471	      || (!exec.out.trim() && looksLikeTap(exec.stderr)),  // TAP went to stderr — same zero-TAP cure (2>&1)
   472	    tapProblems,
   473	    stderr: exec.stderr || '',
   474	    exec: { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error },
   475	  };
   476	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   477	  run.errors = infraErrors(run);
   478	  return run;
   479	}
   480	
   481	// machine-consumable shape for a verify run
   482	function verifyJson(run) {
   483	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   484	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 && run.errors.length === 0;
   485	  const json = projectionless(run, clean);
   486	  if (run.projection) json.projection = run.projection;   // ONLY --change runs carry this field
   487	  return json;
   488	}
   489	
   490	function projectionless(run, clean) {
   491	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   492	  return {
   493	    clean,
   494	    result: run.errors.length ? 'ERROR' : (clean ? 'GREEN' : 'GAPS'),
   495	    errors: run.errors,
   496	    specFiles: run.fileCount,
   497	    exec: run.exec,
   498	    duplicates: run.duplicates,
   499	    boundGreen: v.boundGreen.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   500	    boundRed: v.boundRed.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   501	    unbound: v.unbound,
   502	    orphan: v.orphan.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   503	    unidentified: v.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })),
   504	    unattributedFailures: { count: (v.unattributed || []).length, lines: v.unattributed || [] },
   505	    stderr: run.stderr || '',
   506	  };
   507	}
   508	
   509	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   510	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   511	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   512	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   513	  return value;
   514	}
   515	
   516	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori verify --specs <dir...> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n   or: apriori verify --change <name> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n(--test-cmd may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has a test-cmd row;\n --id-pattern may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has an id-pattern row)';
   517	
   518	function cli(argv) {
   519	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'verify', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   520	    flags: { '--specs': 'multi', '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   521	    const a = { specs: f['--specs'] || [], change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   522	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (SR-52 empty flag)
   523	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   524	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   525	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   526	      return 2;
   527	    }
   528	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   529	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   530	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   531	      a.testCmd = c;
   532	    }
   533	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   534	    const run = verify(a);
   535	    if (run.projection)
   536	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   537	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   538	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   539	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   540	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr }));
   541	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   542	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   543	  });
   544	}
   545

/usr/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba lib/archive-merge.js | sed -n '1,385p'" in /mnt/d/Workbench/misc/apriori-spec-development
 succeeded in 128ms:
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * archive-merge — plain-files native implementation of the archive action (§4).
     4	 * Zero deps — pure Node stdlib. Consumes the same delta format the OpenSpec adapter used
     5	 * (## ADDED/MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED Requirements), so it is the adapter-free path.
     6	 *
     7	 * 3.1: also home of the merge-family pure helpers shared with `verify --change` —
     8	 * delta discovery, projection building, CAS base stamps, realpath containment.
     9	 */
    10	const fs = require('fs');
    11	const path = require('path');
    12	const crypto = require('crypto');
    13	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    14	const { configCas, configCasProblem } = require('./resolve');
    15	
    16	const REQ_RE = /^###\s+Requirement:\s+(.+?)\s*$([\s\S]*?)(?=^###\s+Requirement:|$(?![\s\S]))/gm;
    17	const SECTION_SPLIT_RE = /^##\s+(ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED)\s+Requirements\s*$/m;
    18	// the exact marker merge() injects on REMOVED; heading LINE of a deprecated block
    19	const DEPRECATED_RE = /^###\s+Requirement:.*_deprecated \(superseded by [^)]*\)_/;
    20	// a whole deprecated block (heading line + body up to the next requirement)
    21	const DEPRECATED_BLOCK_RE = /^###[ \t]+Requirement:[^\n]*_deprecated \(superseded by [^)]*\)_[^\n]*$[\s\S]*?(?=^###[ \t]+Requirement:|$(?![\s\S]))/gm;
    22	
    23	function stripDeprecatedBlocks(text) { return text.replace(DEPRECATED_BLOCK_RE, ''); }
    24	
    25	// name -> full block (heading + body), in document order; duplicates reported (first block wins)
    26	function parseRequirementsStrict(text) {
    27	  const map = new Map();
    28	  const duplicates = [];
    29	  let m;
    30	  REQ_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    31	  while ((m = REQ_RE.exec(text)) !== null) {
    32	    const name = m[1].trim();
    33	    if (map.has(name)) duplicates.push(name);
    34	    else map.set(name, m[0].replace(/\s+$/, '') + '\n');
    35	  }
    36	  return { map, duplicates };
    37	}

    39	function parseRequirements(text) { return parseRequirementsStrict(text).map; }

    41	// ---- CAS base stamps -------------------------------------------------------
    42	
    43	// sha256 over content with line endings normalized — a delta authored on Windows
    44	// must fingerprint identically to the same store checked out with LF
    45	function fingerprint(text) {
    46	  const norm = text.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n');
    47	  return 'sha256:' + crypto.createHash('sha256').update(norm, 'utf8').digest('hex');
    48	}
    49	
    50	const STAMP_LINE_RE = /^<!--\s*apriori-base:\s*(\S+)\s*-->\s*$/gm;
    51	// any standalone comment line ATTEMPTING to be a stamp — a malformed attempt must be an error,
    52	// never silently treated as "unstamped" (that would quietly disable CAS)
    53	const STAMP_ATTEMPT_RE = /^\s*<!--(?=[^>]*apriori-base)[^>]*-->\s*$/gm;
    54	
    55	// → { stamp: 'sha256:<hex>' | 'new' | null, problems: [] }
    56	// Exactly one stamp line, before the first delta section, well-formed digest.
    57	function parseStamp(text) {
    58	  const problems = [];
    59	  const sectionIdx = text.search(SECTION_SPLIT_RE);
    60	  STAMP_ATTEMPT_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    61	  let m;
    62	  while ((m = STAMP_ATTEMPT_RE.exec(text)) !== null) {
    63	    STAMP_LINE_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    64	    if (!STAMP_LINE_RE.test(m[0].trim())) problems.push(`malformed apriori-base stamp line: ${m[0].trim()}`);
    65	  }
    66	  const found = [];
    67	  STAMP_LINE_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    68	  while ((m = STAMP_LINE_RE.exec(text)) !== null) found.push({ value: m[1], index: m.index });
    69	  if (!found.length) return { stamp: null, problems };
    70	  if (found.length > 1) problems.push('carries more than one apriori-base stamp line');
    71	  for (const f of found) {
    72	    if (sectionIdx >= 0 && f.index > sectionIdx) problems.push('apriori-base stamp appears after the first delta section heading');
    73	    if (f.value !== 'new' && !/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(f.value)) problems.push(`malformed apriori-base stamp '${f.value}'`);
    74	  }
    75	  return { stamp: problems.length ? null : found[0].value, problems };
    76	}
    77	
    78	// ---- delta parsing ---------------------------------------------------------
    79	
    80	// per-line forms of the shared regexes (no /m — the walker feeds one normalized line at a time)
    81	const SECTION_LINE_RE = /^##\s+(ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED)\s+Requirements\s*$/;
    82	const H2_LINE_RE = /^##\s/;
    83	const REQ_LINE_RE = /^###\s+Requirement:\s+(.+?)\s*$/;
    84	const SCENARIO_LINE_RE = /^####\s+Scenario:/;
    85	const RENAME_LINE_RE = /^\s*-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(.+?)\s*$/;
    86	const STAMP_ATTEMPT_LINE_RE = /^\s*<!--(?=[^>]*apriori-base)[^>]*-->\s*$/;
    87	const STAMP_STRICT_LINE_RE = /^<!--\s*apriori-base:\s*(\S+)\s*-->\s*$/;
    88	const FENCE_LINE_RE = /^\s*```/;
    89	
    90	// strict form: a sequential, fully-consuming line walker — every line (outside
    91	// code fences, which are opaque) belongs to exactly one legal construct, and
    92	// anything else is a problems[] entry carrying its 1-based line number.
    93	// Well-formed deltas parse byte-identically to the old split()-based grammar.
    94	function parseDeltaStrict(text) {
    95	  const problems = [];
    96	  const buckets = { ADDED: new Map(), MODIFIED: new Map(), REMOVED: new Map(), RENAMED: [] };
    97	  const seen = new Map();               // name -> first section carrying it
    98	  const lines = text.split('\n').map((l) => (l.endsWith('\r') ? l.slice(0, -1) : l));
    99	
   100	  let state = 'FILE_PREAMBLE';          // | 'IN_SECTION' | 'IN_REQUIREMENT' | 'SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED'
   101	  let kind = null;                      // current section kind while IN_SECTION/IN_REQUIREMENT
   102	  let blockName = null, blockLines = null, blockDiscard = false;
   103	  let inFence = false;
   104	  let stamp = null, stampSeen = false, stampProblems = false, sectionSeen = false;
   105	
   106	  const flush = () => {
   107	    if (blockLines === null) return;
   108	    if (!blockDiscard) buckets[kind].set(blockName, blockLines.join('\n').replace(/\s+$/, '') + '\n');
   109	    blockName = null; blockLines = null; blockDiscard = false;
   110	  };
   111	  const stampProblem = (msg) => { problems.push(msg); stampProblems = true; };
   112	
   113	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
   114	    const line = lines[i], n = i + 1;
   115	    if (inFence) {                      // opaque: never structure, never a problem
   116	      if (blockLines !== null) blockLines.push(line);
   117	      if (FENCE_LINE_RE.test(line)) inFence = false;
   118	      continue;
   119	    }
   120	    if (FENCE_LINE_RE.test(line)) {
   121	      inFence = true;
   122	      if (blockLines !== null) blockLines.push(line);
   123	      continue;
   124	    }
   125	    const sec = SECTION_LINE_RE.exec(line);
   126	    if (sec) { flush(); state = 'IN_SECTION'; kind = sec[1]; sectionSeen = true; continue; }
   127	    if (H2_LINE_RE.test(line)) {        // one problem per unrecognized heading; its lines are skipped, not re-homed
   128	      flush();
   129	      problems.push(`line ${n}: unrecognized section heading '${line.trim()}'`);
   130	      state = 'SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED'; kind = null;
   131	      continue;
   132	    }
   133	    if (state === 'SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED') continue;   // covered by the heading's problem — no flood, no attribution (stamps included)
   134	    // stamps are matched in every remaining state so a stray one is never absorbed as body text
   135	    if (STAMP_ATTEMPT_LINE_RE.test(line)) {
   136	      const m = STAMP_STRICT_LINE_RE.exec(line.trim());
   137	      if (!m) { stampProblem(`line ${n}: malformed apriori-base stamp line: ${line.trim()}`); continue; }
   138	      if (stampSeen) { stampProblem(`line ${n}: carries more than one apriori-base stamp line`); continue; }
   139	      stampSeen = true;
   140	      if (sectionSeen) { stampProblem(`line ${n}: apriori-base stamp appears after the first delta section heading`); continue; }
   141	      if (m[1] !== 'new' && !/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(m[1])) { stampProblem(`line ${n}: malformed apriori-base stamp '${m[1]}'`); continue; }
   142	      stamp = m[1];
   143	      continue;
   144	    }
   145	    const req = REQ_LINE_RE.exec(line);
   146	    if (req) {
   147	      if (state === 'FILE_PREAMBLE') { problems.push(`line ${n}: requirement block before any section heading`); continue; }
   148	      if (kind === 'RENAMED') {
   149	        // the whole illegal block is discarded — its body must not be reinterpreted as rename lines
   150	        problems.push(`line ${n}: requirement block inside the RENAMED section`);
   151	        flush();
   152	        state = 'IN_REQUIREMENT'; blockName = null; blockLines = []; blockDiscard = true;
   153	        continue;
   154	      }
   155	      flush();
   156	      state = 'IN_REQUIREMENT';
   157	      blockName = req[1].trim(); blockLines = [line]; blockDiscard = false;
   158	      if (buckets[kind].has(blockName)) {          // first block wins, as before
   159	        problems.push(`line ${n}: duplicate requirement '${blockName}' in the ${kind} section`);
   160	        blockDiscard = true;
   161	      } else if (seen.has(blockName)) {
   162	        problems.push(`line ${n}: duplicate requirement '${blockName}' appears in both ${seen.get(blockName)} and ${kind}`);
   163	      } else seen.set(blockName, kind);
   164	      continue;
   165	    }
   166	    if (state === 'IN_REQUIREMENT') { blockLines.push(line); continue; }   // scenarios and prose are body
   167	    if (SCENARIO_LINE_RE.test(line)) { problems.push(`line ${n}: scenario outside any requirement block`); continue; }
   168	    if (state === 'IN_SECTION' && kind === 'RENAMED') {
   169	      const rn = RENAME_LINE_RE.exec(line);
   170	      if (rn) buckets.RENAMED.push([rn[1].trim(), rn[2].trim()]);
   171	      continue;                          // other lines are free text
   172	    }
   173	    // FILE_PREAMBLE / section preamble free text and blanks are legal
   174	  }
   175	  flush();
   176	  return { delta: buckets, stamp: stampProblems ? null : stamp, problems };
   177	}
   178	
   179	function parseDelta(text) { return parseDeltaStrict(text).delta; }
   180	
   181	// total number of operations a parsed delta carries (0 = nothing recognized/parseable)
   182	function deltaOpCount(delta) {
   183	  return delta.ADDED.size + delta.MODIFIED.size + delta.REMOVED.size + delta.RENAMED.length;
   184	}
   185	
   186	// mutation ops can silently clobber concurrent edits; ADDED-only deltas conflict or no-op instead
   187	function mutationOpCount(delta) {
   188	  return delta.MODIFIED.size + delta.REMOVED.size + delta.RENAMED.length;
   189	}
   190	
   191	// the one warning text every surface shares (WARN grade this minor; 4.0 makes stamps mandatory)
   192	// 4.0: unstamped mutation deltas are DENIED by default; --no-cas or the config row waives visibly
   193	// waiver source, or {problem} when the config itself is broken (deny + name it) — the
   194	// explicit flag stays supreme even over a broken config (the flag is a human's will)
   195	function casWaiver(cwd, noCas) {
   196	  if (noCas) return { waiver: '--no-cas' };
   197	  const problem = configCasProblem(cwd);
   198	  if (problem) return { problem };
   199	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { waiver: 'process-config cas: optional' };
   200	  return {};
   201	}
   202	
   203	function unstampedWarning(suffix) {
   204	  return `unstamped mutation delta ${suffix} — divergence undetectable; run: apriori stamp <store-file>`;
   205	}
   206	
   207	// ---- merge (pure) ----------------------------------------------------------
   208	
   209	// Pure merge. Returns { store: Map, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged }
   210	function merge(storeText, delta, change) {
   211	  const parsed = parseRequirementsStrict(storeText);
   212	  const store = parsed.map;
   213	  const merged = [], modified = [], deprecated = [], renamed = [], conflicts = [], unchanged = [];
   214	  // a corrupt store (same requirement name twice) must never be silently collapsed
   215	  for (const d of parsed.duplicates) conflicts.push(`duplicate requirement '${d}' in the store (corrupt store)`);
   216	  const renamedTargets = new Set();   // names created by THIS run's renames — an ADDED hitting one is a same-delta collision, never a rerun no-op
   217	  // RENAMED first: rename the store block's ID, preserving its content
   218	  for (const [oldName, newName] of (delta.RENAMED || [])) {
   219	    if (!store.has(oldName)) {
   220	      // rerun signature: source already gone AND target present → this rename already happened
   221	      if (store.has(newName)) { unchanged.push(`${oldName} -> ${newName} (already renamed)`); continue; }
   222	      conflicts.push(`RENAMED '${oldName}' has no target in store`); continue;
   223	    }
   224	    if (store.has(newName)) { conflicts.push(`RENAMED target '${newName}' already exists in store`); continue; }
   225	    const block = store.get(oldName);
   226	    const renamedBlock = block.replace(/^###\s+Requirement:\s+.+$/m, `### Requirement: ${newName}`);
   227	    // rebuild the map preserving order, swapping the key at the old position
   228	    const rebuilt = new Map();
   229	    for (const [k, v] of store) rebuilt.set(k === oldName ? newName : k, k === oldName ? renamedBlock : v);
   230	    store.clear();
   231	    for (const [k, v] of rebuilt) store.set(k, v);
   232	    renamed.push(`${oldName} -> ${newName}`);
   233	    renamedTargets.add(newName);
   234	  }
   235	  for (const [name, block] of delta.ADDED) {
   236	    if (store.has(name)) {
   237	      // idempotent rerun: identical content means this delta already merged — a no-op, not a collision.
   238	      // But a name just created by THIS run's RENAMED is a same-delta collision, never a rerun.
   239	      if (!renamedTargets.has(name) && store.get(name).trim() === block.trim()) unchanged.push(name);
   240	      else conflicts.push(`ADDED '${name}' already exists in store`);
   241	    } else { store.set(name, block); merged.push(name); }
   242	  }
   243	  for (const [name, block] of delta.MODIFIED) {
   244	    if (!store.has(name)) conflicts.push(`MODIFIED '${name}' has no target in store`);
   245	    // already applied (rerun signature) — same trim comparison the ADDED no-op uses
   246	    else if (store.get(name).trim() === block.trim()) unchanged.push(name);
   247	    else { store.set(name, block); modified.push(name); }
   248	  }
   249	  for (const name of delta.REMOVED.keys()) {
   250	    if (!store.has(name)) {
   251	      // rerun signature: already deprecated BY THIS CHANGE → no-op; by another change → real collision
   252	      const depByThis = `${name}  _deprecated (superseded by ${change})_`;
   253	      if (store.has(depByThis)) { unchanged.push(`${name} (already deprecated)`); continue; }
   254	      const depOther = [...store.keys()].find((k) => k.startsWith(`${name}  _deprecated (superseded by `));
   255	      if (depOther) { conflicts.push(`REMOVED '${name}' was already deprecated by a different change ('${depOther}')`); continue; }
   256	      conflicts.push(`REMOVED '${name}' has no target in store`);
   257	    } else {
   258	      const block = store.get(name);
   259	      const nl = block.indexOf('\n');
   260	      store.set(name, block.slice(0, nl) + `  _deprecated (superseded by ${change})_` + block.slice(nl));
   261	      deprecated.push(name);
   262	    }
   263	  }
   264	  return { store, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged };
   265	}
   266	
   267	function renderStore(storeText, store) {
   268	  const idx = storeText.indexOf('### Requirement:');
   269	  const header = idx >= 0 ? storeText.slice(0, idx) : '';
   270	  return header + [...store.values()].join('\n');
   271	}
   272	
   273	// ---- paths and discovery ---------------------------------------------------
   274	
   275	// Realpath containment: target (followed through symlinks; not-yet-existing targets judged
   276	// by their nearest existing ancestor) must resolve STRICTLY inside root. Fail-closed.
   277	function containsReal(root, target) {
   278	  let realRoot;
   279	  try { realRoot = fs.realpathSync(root); } catch { return false; }
   280	  let cur = path.resolve(target);
   281	  const rest = [];
   282	  while (!fs.existsSync(cur)) {
   283	    const parent = path.dirname(cur);
   284	    if (parent === cur) return false;
   285	    rest.unshift(path.basename(cur));
   286	    cur = parent;
   287	  }
   288	  let realCur;
   289	  try { realCur = fs.realpathSync(cur); } catch { return false; }
   290	  const full = rest.length ? path.join(realCur, ...rest) : realCur;
   291	  return full !== realRoot && (full + path.sep).startsWith(realRoot + path.sep);
   292	}
   293	
   294	function mdFilesUnder(dir) {
   295	  const out = [];
   296	  const walk = (d) => {
   297	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   298	      const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   299	      // Dirents reflect lstat: a symlink is neither file nor directory. Follow it explicitly —
   300	      // silently skipping a symlinked dir would hide deltas (fail-open); containment judges the target.
   301	      let isDir = e.isDirectory();
   302	      if (e.isSymbolicLink()) {
   303	        try { isDir = fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { isDir = false; /* broken link: fall through, read fails loudly */ }
   304	      }
   305	      if (isDir) walk(p);
   306	      else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
   307	    }
   308	  };
   309	  if (fs.existsSync(dir)) walk(dir);
   310	  return out.sort();
   311	}
   312	
   313	// Discover a change's delta files. → { files: [{abs, suffix}], errors: [] } — errors are
   314	// VALIDATION class (exit 2 on both surfaces): bad name, missing dir, escape, zero files.
   315	function discoverDeltas(changesDirAbs, name) {
   316	  const errors = [];
   317	  const vName = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(name);
   318	  if (!vName.ok) return { files: [], errors: [`invalid change name '${name}' (${vName.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`] };
   319	  const changeDir = path.join(changesDirAbs, name);
   320	  if (!fs.existsSync(changeDir)) return { files: [], errors: [`change '${name}' not found under ${changesDirAbs}`] };
   321	  if (!containsReal(changesDirAbs, changeDir)) return { files: [], errors: [`change path escapes ${changesDirAbs}: ${changeDir}`] };
   322	  const specsDir = path.join(changeDir, 'specs');
   323	  // a symlinked specs/ pointing outside would smuggle its target in as the containment ROOT —
   324	  // require specs/ itself to really live inside the change dir before trusting it as a root
   325	  if (fs.existsSync(specsDir) && !containsReal(changeDir, specsDir))
   326	    return { files: [], errors: [`specs dir escapes the change dir (symlink?): ${specsDir}`] };
   327	  const all = mdFilesUnder(specsDir);
   328	  if (!all.length) return { files: [], errors: [`no delta spec files (*.md) found under ${specsDir}`] };
   329	  const files = [];
   330	  for (const abs of all) {
   331	    if (!containsReal(specsDir, abs)) { errors.push(`delta path escapes the change root: ${abs}`); continue; }
   332	    files.push({ abs, suffix: path.relative(specsDir, abs).split(path.sep).join('/') });
   333	  }
   334	  return { files, errors };
   335	}
   336	
   337	// Build the projection: apply every delta to its mapped store file IN MEMORY.
   338	// → { texts: Map<suffix, projectedText>, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches,
   339	//     hygiene, validation } — texts covers ALL store files (pass-through + projected).
   340	function buildProjection(storeRoot, deltaFiles, change) {
   341	  const texts = new Map(), perModule = new Map(), modules = [];
   342	  const conflicts = [], casMismatches = [], hygiene = [], validation = [];
   343	  const unstampedMutations = [], notes = [], repaired = [];
   344	  for (const abs of mdFilesUnder(storeRoot))
   345	    texts.set(path.relative(storeRoot, abs).split(path.sep).join('/'), fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8'));
   346	  for (const { abs, suffix } of deltaFiles) {
   347	    modules.push(suffix);
   348	    const target = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   349	    if (!containsReal(storeRoot, target)) { validation.push(`store target escapes ${storeRoot}: ${target}`); continue; }
   350	    const raw = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8');
   351	    if (!raw.trim()) { hygiene.push(`empty delta file: ${abs}`); continue; }
   352	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(raw);
   353	    if (problems.length) { hygiene.push(...problems.map((p) => `${abs}: ${p}`)); continue; }
   354	    if (deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) { hygiene.push(`parsed 0 delta operations from ${abs} — it has content but no recognized operations`); continue; }
   355	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) unstampedMutations.push(suffix);   // WARN grade (callers print)
   356	    const storeText = texts.get(suffix) || '';
   357	    if (stamp) {
   358	      const actual = texts.has(suffix) ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   359	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   360	        // rerun repair: a mismatch whose every op is already applied is the resumed-rerun
   361	        // signature — divergence with ANY real pending op still fails exactly as before
   362	        const probe = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   363	        const pending = probe.merged.length + probe.modified.length + probe.deprecated.length + probe.renamed.length;
   364	        if (pending === 0 && probe.conflicts.length === 0) {
   365	          notes.push(`${suffix}: stamp mismatch but the delta is already fully applied — rerun accepted`);
   366	          perModule.set(suffix, probe);
   367	          repaired.push(suffix);
   368	          continue;                                          // contributes no writes
   369	        }
   370	        casMismatches.push(`base mismatch for ${target}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual}`); continue;
   371	      }
   372	    }
   373	    const r = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   374	    perModule.set(suffix, r);
   375	    if (r.conflicts.length) { conflicts.push(...r.conflicts.map((c) => `${suffix}: ${c}`)); continue; }
   376	    texts.set(suffix, renderStore(storeText, r.store));
   377	  }
   378	  modules.sort();
   379	  return { texts, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches, hygiene, validation, unstampedMutations, notes, repaired };
   380	}

    27	  for (const key of ['change', 'tier', 'track', 'lineage', 'current-step']) {
    28	    const v = state[key];
    29	    if (v === undefined || v === '') return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: required key '${key}' missing` };
    30	    if (v.includes('<') || v.includes('>')) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: '${key}' is an unfilled placeholder (${v})` };
    31	  }
    32	  if (state.change !== name) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'change' is '${state.change}', expected '${name}'` };
    33	  if (!STEP_ENUM.includes(state['current-step'])) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'current-step' '${state['current-step']}' not in the legal vocabulary` };
    34	  if (!TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier)) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'tier' '${state.tier}' not in {trivial, medium, large}` };
    35	  return { id: 'C3', status: 'pass', detail: `legal (tier ${state.tier}, ${state['current-step']})` };
    36	}
    37	
    38	function checkTasks(dir, tier) {
    39	  const p = path.join(dir, 'tasks.md');
    40	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    41	    return tier === 'trivial'
    42	      ? { id: 'C2', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no tasks.md — trivial tier has no STEP2' }
    43	      : { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md missing at ${p}` };
    44	  }
    45	  const open = (fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8').match(/^\s*-\s\[\s\]/gm) || []).length;
    46	  return open
    47	    ? { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md has ${open} unchecked box(es)` }
    48	    : { id: 'C2', status: 'pass', detail: 'all tasks checked' };
    49	}
    50	
    51	// the ledger vocabulary, one classifier shared by C4 and the corpus test (GT-15).
    52	// leading token, case-insensitive; rejected-verified BEFORE rejected (alternation order).
    53	const STATUS_RE = /^(open|fixed|verified|rejected-verified|rejected|advisory-acked|waived)\b/i;
    54	function classifyStatus(status) {
    55	  const m = STATUS_RE.exec(status);
    56	  if (!m) return { legal: false, terminal: false, needsReason: false, hasReason: false, isWaived: false };
    57	  const tok = m[1].toLowerCase();
    58	  const needsReason = tok === 'rejected' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived';
    59	  return {
    60	    legal: true,
    61	    terminal: tok === 'verified' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived' || tok === 'advisory-acked',
    62	    needsReason,
    63	    hasReason: !needsReason || /\w/.test(status.slice(m[0].length)),
    64	    isWaived: tok === 'waived',
    65	    token: tok,
    66	  };
    67	}
    68	
    69	const escapeRe = (s) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    70	
    71	// gates: entries from a flow-state text — the block runs from the unindented `gates:` line
    72	// to the next unindented top-level key; entries start `- `, continuation lines attach.
    73	function gatesEntries(flowText) {
    74	  const start = flowText.search(/^gates:/m);
    75	  if (start < 0) return [];
    76	  const rest = flowText.slice(start + 'gates:'.length);
    77	  const end = rest.search(/^[A-Za-z][\w-]*:/m);
    78	  const block = end < 0 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
    79	  const entries = [];
    80	  for (const line of block.split('\n')) {
    81	    if (/^\s*-\s/.test(line)) entries.push(line.trim());
    82	    else if (entries.length && line.trim()) entries[entries.length - 1] += ' ' + line.trim();
    83	  }
    84	  return entries;
    85	}
    86	
    87	// a waive is a HUMAN act: one single gates: entry must carry the row ID as an exact
    88	// token (LS-1 never matches inside LS-10) AND the word waive/waived.
    89	function waiveEvidence(flowText, id) {
    90	  const idRe = new RegExp(`(^|[^A-Za-z0-9-])${escapeRe(id)}([^A-Za-z0-9-]|$)`);
    91	  return gatesEntries(flowText).some((e) => idRe.test(e) && /waiv/i.test(e));
    92	}
    93	
    94	function checkLedger(tier, stage, dir) {
    95	  const p = path.join(dir, 'review', 'issues.md');
    96	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    97	    return tier === 'trivial'
    98	      ? { id: 'C4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no ledger — trivial tier may never open one' }
    99	      : { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: `ledger missing at ${p}` };
   100	  }
   101	  const rows = parseLedger(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
   102	  const fsPath = dir ? path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md') : null;
   103	  const flowText = fsPath && fs.existsSync(fsPath) ? fs.readFileSync(fsPath, 'utf8') : '';
   104	  const bad = [];
   105	  for (const r of rows) {
   106	    const c = classifyStatus(r.status);
   107	    if (!c.legal) { bad.push(`${r.id}: '${r.status}' is not in the ledger vocabulary`); continue; }
   108	    if (c.needsReason && !c.hasReason) { bad.push(`${r.id} is ${c.token} without a reason`); continue; }
   109	    if (c.isWaived && !waiveEvidence(flowText, r.id)) {
   110	      bad.push(`${r.id} is waived without a gates: entry recording the human decision`); continue;
   111	    }
   112	    if (c.token === 'open') { bad.push(`${r.id} is open`); continue; }
   113	    if (stage === 'archived' && !c.terminal) {
   114	      bad.push(c.token === 'fixed'
   115	        ? `${r.id} is fixed but never verified — reviewer must verify, or the human waives it`
   116	        : `${r.id} is rejected without reviewer concurrence — flip to rejected-verified, or the human waives it`);
   117	    }
   118	  }
   119	  return bad.length
   120	    ? { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: bad.join('; ') }
   121	    : { id: 'C4', status: 'pass', detail: `${rows.length} row(s), none blocking` };
   122	}
   123	
   124	// the bundle review/ dir is the shared evidence root for C4 and C5 — when present it must be
   125	// a REAL contained directory (symlinked/escaping/non-dir entries block, never read through)
   126	function reviewDirDefect(dir) {
   127	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   128	  let st;
   129	  try { st = fs.lstatSync(rd); } catch { return null; }  // lstat, not exists: a dangling symlink is a defect, not absence
   130	  if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return `review is a symlink: ${rd}`;
   131	  if (!st.isDirectory()) return `review is not a directory: ${rd}`;
   132	  if (!containsReal(dir, rd)) return `review escapes the change dir: ${rd}`;
   133	  return null;
   134	}
   135	
   136	function checkEvidence(dir) {
   137	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   138	  const docs = [];
   139	  if (fs.existsSync(rd)) {
   140	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(rd, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   141	      if (!e.name.endsWith('.md') || e.name === 'issues.md' || /-raw(\.|$)/.test(e.name.replace(/\.md$/, ''))) continue;
   142	      if (e.name.includes('-raw')) continue;
   143	      const p = path.join(rd, e.name);
   144	      if (fs.lstatSync(p).isSymbolicLink())
   145	        return { id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: `evidence doc is a symlink: ${e.name}` };
   146	      if (!fs.lstatSync(p).isFile()) continue;
   147	      docs.push(p);
   148	    }
   149	  }
   150	  const missing = [];
   151	  let verdictDocs = 0;
   152	  for (const doc of docs) {
   153	    if (!/^VERDICT:/m.test(fs.readFileSync(doc, 'utf8'))) continue;
   154	    verdictDocs++;
   155	    const stem = path.basename(doc).replace(/\.md$/, '');
   156	    const hasRaw = fs.existsSync(rd) && fs.readdirSync(rd).some((n) => {
   157	      if (!n.startsWith(stem + '-raw')) return false;
   158	      const rp = path.join(rd, n);
   159	      return fs.lstatSync(rp).isFile();
   160	    });
   161	    if (!hasRaw) missing.push(path.basename(doc));
   162	  }
   163	  if (missing.length) return { id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: `verdict doc(s) without a raw archive: ${missing.join(', ')}` };
   164	  return { id: 'C5', status: 'pass', detail: `${verdictDocs} review doc(s), every verdict has raw evidence` };
   165	}
   166	
   167	function checkKb(cwd, dir) {
   168	  const specsDir = path.join(dir, 'specs');
   169	  const modules = new Set();
   170	  const walk = (d, base) => {
   171	    if (!fs.existsSync(d)) return;
   172	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   173	      const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   174	      if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p, base);
   175	      else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) modules.add(path.relative(base, p).split(path.sep)[0]);
   176	    }
   177	  };
   178	  walk(specsDir, specsDir);
   179	  if (!modules.size) return { id: 'C6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no delta specs to map' };
   180	
   181	  // build the module → truth index (c6-truth-binding): a truth doc binds by its
   182	  // declared store-module (default = basename), never by a filename assumption
   183	  const truthDir = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'truth');
   184	  const index = new Map();                 // module → { text, sourceFiles, basename }
   185	  const conflicts = new Map();             // module → "declared by A and B"
   186	  if (fs.existsSync(truthDir)) {
   187	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(truthDir).sort()) {
   188	      if (!e.endsWith('.md')) continue;
   189	      const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(truthDir, e), 'utf8');
   190	      const h2 = text.search(/^##\s/m);
   191	      const header = h2 >= 0 ? text.slice(0, h2) : text;
   192	      const smM = header.match(/^store-module:\s*(.+)$/m);
   193	      const sfM = header.match(/^source-files:\s*(.+)$/m);
   194	      const mods = smM ? smM[1].trim().split(/\s+/) : [e.replace(/\.md$/, '')];
   195	      const sourceFiles = sfM ? sfM[1].trim().split(/\s+/) : null;   // null = default lib/<module>.js
   196	      for (const m of mods) {
   197	        if (index.has(m)) conflicts.set(m, `${m}: declared by both ${index.get(m).basename} and ${e}`);
   198	        else index.set(m, { text, sourceFiles, basename: e });
   199	      }
   200	    }
   201	  }
   202	  const stripFencesKb = (t) => t.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, '');
   203	  const blocked = [], notes = [];
   204	  let checked = 0;
   205	  for (const m of [...modules].sort()) {
   206	    if (conflicts.has(m)) { blocked.push(conflicts.get(m)); continue; }
   207	    const entry = index.get(m);
   208	    if (!entry) { notes.push(`${m}: no truth doc`); continue; }
   209	    const noFence = stripFencesKb(entry.text);
   210	    const sc = noFence.match(/^source-commit:\s+(\S+)/m);
   211	    if (!sc) {
   212	      const attempt = noFence.split('\n').some((l) => l.includes('source-commit:') && !/^source-commit:\s+\S+/.test(l));
   213	      notes.push(attempt
   214	        ? `${m}: source-commit is not in the canonical fence-outside line-start form 'source-commit: <ref>'`
   215	        : `${m}: truth doc has no source-commit`);
   216	      continue;
   217	    }
   218	    const ref = sc[1];
   219	    const explicit = entry.sourceFiles !== null;
   220	    const declared = explicit ? entry.sourceFiles : [`lib/${m}.js`];
   221	    // an explicit declaration is a complete promise: every token must be verifiable
   222	    const usable = [];
   223	    let tokenBad = null;
   224	    for (const rel of declared) {
   225	      // an explicit token is a repo-relative promise: reject absolute/backslash/empty/. or .. segments
   226	      if (explicit && (rel === '' || path.isAbsolute(rel) || rel.includes('\\') ||
   227	          rel.split('/').some((seg) => seg === '' || seg === '.' || seg === '..'))) {
   228	        tokenBad = `${m}: malformed source-files token (must be a normalized repo-relative path): ${rel}`; break;
   229	      }
   230	      const abs = path.join(cwd, rel);
   231	      let st = null; try { st = fs.lstatSync(abs); } catch { /* absent */ }
   232	      if (!st) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token missing: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   233	      if (st.isSymbolicLink()) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token is a symlink: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   234	      if (!containsReal(cwd, abs)) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token escapes the repo: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   235	      if (!st.isFile() && !st.isDirectory()) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token is neither file nor directory: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   236	      usable.push(rel);
   237	    }
   238	    if (tokenBad) { blocked.push(tokenBad); continue; }
   239	    if (!usable.length) { notes.push(`${m}: no ${declared.join(' / ')} to compare`); continue; }
   240	    const g = spawnSync('git', ['-C', cwd, 'log', '--oneline', `${ref}..HEAD`, '--', ...usable], { encoding: 'utf8' });

   252	function checkBinding(cwd, name, stage, testCmd, idPattern) {
   253	  const run = stage === 'in-flight'
   254	    ? verify({ change: name, cwd, testCmd, idPattern })
   255	    : verify({ specs: [path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs')], cwd, testCmd, idPattern });
   256	  if (run.errors.length) return { infra: run.errors };
   257	  const v = run.verdict;
   258	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0;
   259	  if (clean) return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'pass', detail: `verify GREEN (${stage})` }, projection: run.projection };
   260	  const parts = [];
   261	  for (const [label, arr] of [['red', v.boundRed], ['unbound', v.unbound], ['orphan', v.orphan], ['unidentified', v.unidentified], ['unattributed-failures', v.unattributed || []], ['duplicate-IDs', run.duplicates]])
   262	    if (arr.length) parts.push(`${arr.length} ${label}`);
   263	  return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'blocked', detail: `verify GAPS: ${parts.join(', ')}` }, projection: run.projection };
   264	}

   267	// C7: unstamped mutation deltas are denied by default — a waiver is always visible (GT-16).
   268	// Archived stage: the deltas are already merged; there is nothing left to stamp-check.
   269	function checkCas(projection, stage, noCas, cwd) {
   270	  if (stage === 'archived') return { id: 'C7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'deltas already merged' };
   271	  const um = (projection && projection.unstampedMutations) || [];
   272	  if (!um.length) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'every mutation delta is stamped' };  // nothing to consult — silent pass (GT-16)
   273	  if (noCas) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (--no-cas)' };
   274	  const problem = configCasProblem(cwd);                       // consumption-time only
   275	  if (problem) return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked', detail: `${problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver` };
   276	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (process-config cas: optional)' };
   277	  return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked',
   278	    detail: `unstamped mutation delta(s): ${um.join(', ')} — run: apriori stamp <store-file> (or waive with --no-cas / a process-config cas: optional row)` };
   279	}
   280	
   281	// The whole evaluation. → { code, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors }
   282	function runGate(opts) {
   283	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
   284	  const res = { code: 0, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'PASS', blocked: 0, errors: [], change: opts.change || null };
   285	  if (!opts.change) return err(res, USAGE);   // the ONE usage constant — every path lists the same flags
   286	  const nameCheck = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(opts.change);
   287	  if (!nameCheck.ok) return err(res, `invalid change name '${opts.change}' (${nameCheck.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`);
   288	  const loc = resolveChange(cwd, opts.change);
   289	  if (loc.error) return err(res, loc.error);
   290	  res.stage = loc.stage;
   291	  const flowPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'flow-state.md');
   292	  if (!fs.existsSync(flowPath)) return err(res, `no readable flow-state.md at ${flowPath} — tier-aware checks are impossible`);
   293	  let state;
   294	  try { state = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(flowPath, 'utf8')); }
   295	  catch (e) { return err(res, `flow-state unreadable: ${e.message}`); }
   296	  let testCmd = opts.testCmd;
   297	  if (!testCmd) {
   298	    const c = configTestCmd(cwd);
   299	    if (c && c.error) return err(res, `verify: ${c.error}`);
   300	    testCmd = c;
   301	  }
   302	  if (!testCmd) return err(res, 'no test command: pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to apriori/process-config.md');
   303	
   304	  const b = checkBinding(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, testCmd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   305	  if (b.infra) { for (const e of b.infra) res.errors.push(`verify: ${e}`); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
   306	  res.checks.push(b.check);
   307	  const tier = TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier) ? state.tier : null;
   308	  res.checks.push(checkTasks(loc.dir, tier));
   309	  res.checks.push(checkFlowState(state, opts.change));
   310	  const rdDefect = reviewDirDefect(loc.dir);
   311	  if (rdDefect) {
   312	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   313	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   314	  } else {
   315	    res.checks.push(checkLedger(tier, loc.stage, loc.dir));
   316	    res.checks.push(checkEvidence(loc.dir));
   317	  }
   318	
   319	  res.checks.push(checkKb(cwd, loc.dir));
   320	  res.checks.push(checkCas(b.projection, loc.stage, !!opts.noCas, cwd));
   321	
   322	  res.blocked = res.checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'blocked').length;
   323	  res.result = res.blocked ? 'BLOCKED' : 'PASS';
   324	  res.code = res.blocked ? 1 : 0;
   325	  return res;
   326	}
   327	
   328	function toJson(res) {
   329	  return { change: res.change, stage: res.stage, checks: res.checks, result: res.result, blocked: res.blocked, errors: res.errors };
   330	}
   331	
   332	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json] [--no-cas]';
   333	
   334	function cli(argv) {
   335	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'gate', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   336	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
   337	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   338	    const a = { change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   339	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (GT-24 empty flag)
   340	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'], noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   341	    const res = runGate(a);
   342	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   343	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
   344	    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–' };
   345	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
   346	    if (res.code !== 2) console.log(res.code === 0 ? `\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}` : `\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
   347	    return res.code;
   348	  });
   349	}

apriori/specs/update/spec.md:25:`apriori update` SHALL consult `apriori/managed.json` (`{ "version": 1, "files": { "<relative path>": "sha256:<64hex>" } }`, hashes over the exact bytes the tool last wrote, never line-ending-normalized) and refresh a candidate file only when the manifest lists it AND its current content hash equals the recorded hash; after a real write the entry is updated to the new content's hash. A listed file with a differing hash is `modified` — skipped with a warning naming the file and the cure (delete it and rerun `apriori init --tools <t>`). An existing file with no entry is `unmanaged` — skipped, never written. A listed file absent on disk is `missing` — skipped (recreating is init's job). Skip-only runs still exit 0 and name every skip. When no manifest exists (pre-manifest project), update adopts what it can prove: the runbook unconditionally (refreshed as today), and each command file only if its content hash equals the current template or a known shipped generation (the package embeds every generation's sha256; a membership test over the live `templates/command.md` keeps the list honest); unproven content is `unmanaged`. The adoption pass writes the manifest. A manifest that exists but is unreadable, unparseable, missing `files`, hash-malformed, `version !== 1`, or carrying a key that is absolute, contains `..`, or is outside the allowed refresh-target set (`apriori/runbook.md`, `TOOLS[*].command`) SHALL make update exit nonzero naming the defect before touching any target; every write additionally requires realpath containment under the project root. `--dry-run` produces the identical report and writes nothing — the manifest included.
apriori/specs/cli/spec.md:45:Every subcommand SHALL parse argv through one shared helper with three uniform behaviors: `--help`/`-h` prints that subcommand's usage on stdout and exits 0 (checked before any other validation); an unknown `-`-prefixed token prints `unknown flag` naming it plus the usage on stderr and exits 2 — nothing is silently ignored; positional arity is enforced (`new` and `stamp` exactly one, all others zero). Known-flag semantics: `value` flags consume exactly the next token (missing → exit 2 naming the flag; repeats last-write-wins), `multi` flags consume until the next `-`-prefixed token (empty set → exit 2 naming the flag; repeats accumulate), boolean flags are idempotent. Success paths of all documented invocations are unchanged; the three declared behavior changes are: `new` extras now error (previously ignored), `stamp --foo` is now an unknown flag (previously its positional), and multi consumption stops at single-dash tokens (previously `--`-only).

apriori/specs/archive-merge/spec.md:137:### Requirement: unstamped mutation deltas are denied by default and reruns of applied stamps repair
apriori/specs/archive-merge/spec.md:138:A delta carrying MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED operations without a CAS stamp SHALL be **denied by default on every archive surface** — the high-level `archive --change` form AND the single-file `--store --delta` form: preflight-grade error (exit 1) naming the file and the cure (`apriori stamp <store-file>`), with **nothing written, staged, or moved**. A visible waiver downgrades to the former warn-and-merge behavior: the archive CLI's `--no-cas` flag or a `| cas | optional |` row in `apriori/process-config.md` (the flag wins), and the output names which waiver source applied. ADDED-only deltas stay exempt (the exemption is clobber-focused: they conflict or no-op, never silently overwrite). `verify --change` (the read-only projection) keeps warning without judging; `buildProjection` still returns `unstampedMutations: string[]`. `merge()`'s MODIFIED op SHALL report `unchanged` when the delta block trim-equals the current store block. Archive preflight SHALL classify each stamped delta per file: stamp matches → merge as today; stamp mismatches with EVERY op `unchanged` → a note (`rerun accepted`) and the run proceeds, move included; stamp mismatches with ANY real pending op → error, the whole preflight fails with nothing written or moved — divergence with pending work is never repaired, and notes stay distinct from errors in the diagnostics.
apriori/specs/archive-merge/spec.md:152:#### Scenario: AM-33 an already-applied stamped delta reruns to completion

apriori/specs/archive-merge/spec.md:154:- THEN every mismatched file reports the rerun-accepted note, stores stay byte-identical, the change dir moves, and the exit is 0
apriori/specs/archive-merge/spec.md:165:With `--write` and an explicit `--changes-dir`, `archiveChange` SHALL, AFTER the stores commit and BEFORE the change-dir move, rename every file in `<cwd>/requirement/` whose basename exactly matches one of three anchored patterns for the change — `^<change>-req-v[0-9]+\.md$`, `^<change>-req-final\.md$`, `^<change>-intent-card\.md$` (change name regex-escaped) — into `changes/<name>/requirement/` (created on demand, basenames preserved), so the existing atomic move carries the requirement history; no post-move writes exist. Fail-closed: the source dir must realpath-resolve inside cwd and the destination must pass containment under the changes dir before any read or rename; a MATCHING candidate that is a symlink (lstat) fails the run before the move, naming the symlink and the cure — symlinks are never followed, non-matching entries are ignored. Any staging failure aborts before the move with exit 1 and the existing rerunnable taxonomy (`stores committed but requirement staging failed … — rerun to complete`); a rerun completes staging and the move (stores re-verify as applied, staged files no-op). Zero matches → silent no-op; dry-run and the single-file form never stage. The report carries `staged: <n> requirement file(s) → changes/<name>/requirement/` when n > 0.

apriori/truth/archive-merge.md:13:  - ADDED on existing name: identical trimmed content → `unchanged` (idempotent rerun no-op) UNLESS the name was created by this run's RENAMED (`renamedTargets`) → conflict.
apriori/truth/archive-merge.md:14:  - MODIFIED replaces the whole block; missing target → conflict; delta block trim-equal to the store block → `unchanged` (the rerun/idempotence signature, since cas-enforcement).
apriori/truth/archive-merge.md:16:  - RENAMED with source gone + target present → `unchanged` ("already renamed" rerun signature); source gone + target absent → conflict; target already exists → conflict.
apriori/truth/archive-merge.md:17:  - REMOVED with target gone but `<name>  _deprecated (superseded by <THIS change>)_` present → `unchanged` ("already deprecated" rerun signature); deprecated by a DIFFERENT change → conflict.
apriori/truth/archive-merge.md:38:- CAS enforcement is GRADED since cas-enforcement (3.3.x): an unstamped delta STILL MERGES, but mutation-carrying ones (MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED — ADDED-only exempt, the exemption is clobber-focused) warn on every surface (`unstampedWarning(suffix)`, both archive forms + verify --change stderr) and `buildProjection` returns `unstampedMutations: string[]` (suffixes) that gate C7 denies by default (--no-cas / `| cas | optional |` waive it, visibly). Stamps become mandatory in 4.0. Rerun repair: a stamped file whose base mismatches but whose EVERY op is `unchanged` (the already-applied signature; MODIFIED now reports `unchanged` on trim-equality) downgrades to a `rerun accepted` note in `notes[]`/`repaired[]` and contributes no writes — the move phase completes; a mismatch with ANY real pending op still fails the whole preflight.

apriori/changes/verify-change-scope/review/req-review-v3.md:51:### REQ-8：合法的 RENAMED + MODIFIED 组合无法用单值 `operation` 表达

apriori/changes/verify-change-scope/specs/spec-runner/spec.md:6:On a `--change` run, `verify` SHALL judge its verdict (exit 0/1) on the CHANGE SCOPE only, while reporting the whole projection informatively. The change scope is defined by requirement-block provenance over the projection: the blocks produced or renamed by the delta's ADDED / MODIFIED / RENAMED operations (idempotent reruns included; blocks whose final projected state is deprecated — including rename-then-remove — are excluded), with their scenario occurrences collected at occurrence level. `RENAMED` keeps its store semantics (requirement name only; scenario IDs preserved). The change verdict is GREEN iff every scoped scenario has ≥1 passing test and none red, no scoped occurrence is unidentified, and no scoped scenario ID occurs more than once ACROSS THE WHOLE PROJECTION (a cross-boundary collision is a binding ambiguity of this change; collisions entirely outside the scope go to the store report only). Out-of-scope tests are never ORPHAN for the verdict; a test whose leading ID exists nowhere in the projection is a true orphan, reported in the store report. The store report is a COMPLETE informative evaluation of the whole projection against the same TAP snapshot: `boundRed`, `unbound`, true-`orphan`, `unidentified`, `unattributedFailures`, `duplicates` (each a full list whose length is its count; `boundGreen` as a bare count) — out-of-scope reds, duplicates and unidentified MUST stay visible there. Execution follows a conditional single-run contract: invalid pattern → 0 projection builds / 0 content reads / 0 test spawns / 0 TAP parses (the fail-early order stands); projection or title-batch matcher failure → exactly 1 projection, 0 spawns; the normal and post-TAP-error paths → exactly 1 projection, 1 spawn, 1 parse; both views share the one projection and the one parsed TAP snapshot — the report never re-runs tests. Exit semantics: infra ERROR classes are unchanged; solely for `--change`, a non-zero test-process status that is EXPLAINED by parsed failures (`failCount > 0`) with no infra error no longer forces a non-zero exit — a clean change verdict is GREEN exit 0 (a scoped amendment to the non-zero rule; an unexplained non-zero stays ERROR exit 2). `--specs` runs are byte-identical to before.

apriori/changes/verify-change-scope/specs/spec-runner/spec.md:18:- THEN the modified block's XA-01 and XA-02 are both in scope; the removed block's scenario is not demanded and its lingering test lands in the store report's orphan; the renamed block's XA-01 stays in scope under its unchanged ID; the rename-then-modify final block is in scope with `operations: ["RENAMED","MODIFIED"]`; the rename-then-remove final deprecated block is out of scope
